# Improved Approximation Algorithms for Relational Clustering

关系型聚类改进近似算法

ARYAN ESMAILPOUR, Department of Computer Science, University of Illinois Chicago, USA STAVROS SINTOS, Department of Computer Science, University of Illinois Chicago, USA

艾瑞安·埃斯梅尔普尔，美国伊利诺伊大学芝加哥分校计算机科学系；斯塔夫罗斯·辛托斯，美国伊利诺伊大学芝加哥分校计算机科学系

Clustering plays a crucial role in computer science, facilitating data analysis and problem-solving across numerous fields. By partitioning large datasets into meaningful groups, clustering reveals hidden structures and relationships within the data, aiding tasks such as unsupervised learning, classification, anomaly detection, and recommendation systems. Particularly in relational databases, where data is distributed across multiple tables, efficient clustering is essential yet challenging due to the computational complexity of joining tables. This paper addresses this challenge by introducing efficient algorithms for $k$ -median and $k$ -means clustering on relational data without the need for pre-computing the join query results. For the relational $k$ -median clustering,we propose the first efficient relative approximation algorithm. For the relational $k$ -means clustering, our algorithm significantly improves both the approximation factor and the running time of the known relational $k$ -means clustering algorithms,which suffer either from large constant approximation factors,or expensive running time. Given a join query $\mathbf{q}$ over a database $\mathbf{D}$ of $O\left( N\right)$ tuples,for both $k$ -median and $k$ - means clustering,over the results of $\mathbf{q}$ on $\mathbf{D}$ ,we propose randomized $\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$ -approximation algorithms that run in roughly $O\left( {{k}^{2}{N}^{\text{fhw }}}\right)  + {T}_{\gamma }\left( {k}^{2}\right)$ time,where $\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ is a constant parameter decided by the user,fhw is the fractional hyper-tree width of $\mathbf{q}$ ,while $\gamma$ and ${T}_{\gamma }\left( x\right)$ are respectively the approximation factor and the running time of a traditional clustering algorithm in the standard computational setting over $x$ points.

聚类在计算机科学中扮演着关键角色，它促进跨领域数据分析和问题解决。通过将大型数据集划分为有意义的群组，聚类能揭示数据中隐藏的结构与关联，助力无监督学习、分类、异常检测和推荐系统等任务。尤其在关系型数据库中，数据分散存储于多张表格，由于表连接计算复杂度高，高效聚类既至关重要又颇具挑战。本文针对$k$-中位数和$k$-均值关系型聚类问题，提出无需预计算连接查询结果的高效算法。对于关系型$k$-中位数聚类，我们首次提出高效相对近似算法；对于关系型$k$-均值聚类，我们的算法在近似因子和时间复杂度上显著优于现有方案——现有算法或因近似因子常数过大，或因计算耗时过长而受限。给定包含$O\left( N\right)$个元组的数据库$\mathbf{D}$上的连接查询$\mathbf{q}$，我们针对$\mathbf{q}$在$\mathbf{D}$上的结果，提出随机化$\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$-近似算法，其时间复杂度约为$O\left( {{k}^{2}{N}^{\text{fhw }}}\right)  + {T}_{\gamma }\left( {k}^{2}\right)$，其中$\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$为用户设定的常数参数，fhw是$\mathbf{q}$的分数超树宽度，$\gamma$和${T}_{\gamma }\left( x\right)$分别表示标准计算环境下对$x$个点进行传统聚类时的近似因子和运行时间。

## 1 INTRODUCTION

## 1 引言

Clustering is a fundamental process in computer science, serving as a vital tool for data analysis, pattern recognition, and problem-solving across various domains. Through clustering, large datasets are partitioned into meaningful groups, unveiling hidden structures and relationships within the data. In machine learning, clustering algorithms are used to enable tasks such as classification, anomaly detection, and recommendation systems. Its significance lies in its ability to transform raw data into useful knowledge, empowering researchers, and businesses to make informed decisions.

聚类是计算机科学中的基础性方法，作为数据分析、模式识别和跨领域问题解决的重要工具。通过将海量数据划分为有意义的群组，聚类能揭示数据内部隐藏的结构与关联。在机器学习领域，聚类算法被用于分类、异常检测和推荐系统等任务。其核心价值在于将原始数据转化为有效知识，助力研究人员和企业做出精准决策。

In relational databases, data is gathered and stored across various tables. Each table consists of a set of tuples and two tuples stored in different tables might refer to the same entity. Relational data is decoupled into different relational tables, and we cannot obtain the full data unless all the tables are joined. This setting is quite common in real database management systems (DBMS). As shown in the DB-engines study [1] the vast majority of database systems are relational DBMS. Kaggle surveys [2] demonstrate that most of the learning tasks encountered by data scientists involve relational data. More specifically, ${70}\%$ of database systems are relational DBMS and 65% of the data sets in learning tasks are relational data. As mentioned in [20], relational data is expected to reach $\$ {122.38}$ billion by 2027 [48] in investments.

关系型数据库中的数据分散存储于多张表格。每张表格包含若干元组，不同表格中的元组可能指向同一实体。关系型数据被解耦到不同关系表中，必须通过表连接才能获取完整信息——这种架构在现实数据库管理系统(DBMS)中十分普遍。DB-engines研究[1]显示绝大多数数据库系统采用关系型DBMS，Kaggle调研[2]表明数据科学家65%的学习任务涉及关系型数据。具体而言，${70}\%$的数据库系统属于关系型DBMS，学习任务中65%的数据集为关系型数据。据文献[20]预测，到2027年关系型数据投资规模将达$\$ {122.38}$十亿美元[48]。

In order to explore and process relational data, usually two steps are required: data preparation, and data processing. In the data preparation step, tuples from different tables are joined to construct useful data, while in data processing, an algorithm (for example a clustering algorithm) is performed on the join results to analyze the data. This two-step approach is usually too expensive because the size of the join results can be polynomially larger than the total size of the input tables $\left\lbrack  {{45},{46}}\right\rbrack$ .

关系型数据的探索处理通常需要两个步骤：数据准备与数据处理。数据准备阶段通过连接不同表格的元组构建有效数据集；数据处理阶段则在连接结果上执行算法（如聚类算法）进行分析。这种两阶段方法往往代价高昂，因为连接结果规模可能呈多项式级数大于输入表$\left\lbrack  {{45},{46}}\right\rbrack$的总规模。

---

In this paper,given a (full) conjunctive query and a database instance,we study efficient $k$ - median and $k$ -means clustering on the results of the query without first computing the query results. While there are recent papers $\left\lbrack  {{20},{23},{43}}\right\rbrack$ on designing clustering algorithms on relational data, they usually suffer from i) large additive approximation error, ii) large constant relative approximation error, and iii) expensive running time.

本文在给定(完整)联合查询及数据库实例的前提下，研究如何在不预先计算查询结果的情况下，高效实现查询结果的$k$-中位数与$k$-均值聚类。尽管近期有文献$\left\lbrack  {{20},{23},{43}}\right\rbrack$探讨关系数据聚类算法设计，但这些方法通常存在以下缺陷：i) 较大的加性近似误差；ii) 较高的恒定相对近似误差；iii) 昂贵的运行时间。

Despite recent attention to relational clustering,the problem of efficiently solving relational $k$ - median clustering without additive approximation error remained unsolved. In this paper, we propose the first efficient relative approximation algorithm for the $k$ -median clustering on relational data. Furthermore,by extending our methods,we design an algorithm for the $k$ -means clustering on relational data that dominates (with respect to both the running time and the approximation ratio) all the known relative approximation algorithms.

尽管关系聚类近期受到关注，但如何高效解决无加性近似误差的关系$k$-中位数聚类问题仍未攻克。本文首次提出针对关系数据的$k$-中位数聚类的有效相对近似算法。此外，通过方法扩展，我们设计的$k$-均值聚类算法在运行时间和近似比率上均优于所有已知的相对近似算法。

### 1.1 Notation and problem definition

### 1.1 符号与问题定义

Conjunctive Queries. We are given a database schema R over a set of $d$ attributes A. The database schema $\mathbf{R}$ contains $m$ relations ${R}_{1},\ldots ,{R}_{m}$ . Let ${\mathbf{A}}_{j} \subseteq  \mathbf{A}$ be the set of attributes associated with relation ${R}_{j} \in  \mathbf{R}$ . For an attribute $A \in  \mathbf{A}$ ,let $\operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)$ be the domain of attribute $A$ . We assume that $\operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)  = \mathbb{R}$ for every $A \in  \mathbf{A}$ . Let $\mathbf{D}$ be a database instance over the database schema $\mathbf{R}$ . For simplicity,we assume that each relation ${R}_{j}$ contains $N$ tuples in $\mathbf{D}$ . Throughout the paper, we consider data complexity i.e., $m$ and $d$ are constants,while $N$ is a large integer. We use ${R}_{j}$ to denote both the relation and the set of tuples from D stored in the relation. For a subset of attributes $B \subseteq  \mathbf{A}$ and a set $Y \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,let ${\pi }_{B}\left( Y\right)$ be the set containing the projection of the tuples in $Y$ onto the attributes $B$ . Notice that two different tuples in $Y$ might have the same projection on $B$ ,however, ${\pi }_{B}\left( Y\right)$ is defined as a set,so the projected tuple is stored once. We also define the multi-set ${\bar{\pi }}_{B}\left( Y\right)$ so that if for two tuples ${t}_{1},{t}_{2} \in  Y$ it holds that ${\pi }_{B}\left( {t}_{1}\right)  = {\pi }_{B}\left( {t}_{2}\right)$ ,then the tuple ${\pi }_{B}\left( {t}_{1}\right)$ exists more than once in ${\bar{\pi }}_{B}\left( Y\right)$ .

联合查询。给定定义在属性集A上的数据库模式R，该模式$\mathbf{R}$包含$m$个关系${R}_{1},\ldots ,{R}_{m}$。设${\mathbf{A}}_{j} \subseteq  \mathbf{A}$为关系${R}_{j} \in  \mathbf{R}$的属性集，属性$A \in  \mathbf{A}$的域记为$\operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)$，并假定对任意$A \in  \mathbf{A}$都有$\operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)  = \mathbb{R}$。设$\mathbf{D}$为数据库模式$\mathbf{R}$的实例，为简化起见，假定每个关系${R}_{j}$在$\mathbf{D}$中包含$N$个元组。本文考虑数据复杂度，即视$m$和$d$为常数，而$N$为大整数。符号${R}_{j}$既表示关系，也表示该关系中存储的元组集合。对于属性子集$B \subseteq  \mathbf{A}$和集合$Y \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，定义${\pi }_{B}\left( Y\right)$为$Y$中元组在$B$属性上的投影集合（注意：不同元组在$B$上可能有相同投影，但${\pi }_{B}\left( Y\right)$作为集合仅存储一次投影结果）。同时定义多重集${\bar{\pi }}_{B}\left( Y\right)$，若元组${t}_{1},{t}_{2} \in  Y$满足${\pi }_{B}\left( {t}_{1}\right)  = {\pi }_{B}\left( {t}_{2}\right)$，则${\pi }_{B}\left( {t}_{1}\right)$在${\bar{\pi }}_{B}\left( Y\right)$中存在多次。

Following the related work on relational clustering [20, 23, 43], we are given a full conjunctive query (join query) $\mathbf{q} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{1} \bowtie  \ldots  \bowtie  {R}_{m}$ . The set of results of a join query $\mathbf{q}$ over the database instance $\mathrm{D}$ is defined as $\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  = \left\{  {t \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d} \mid  \forall j \in  \left\lbrack  {1,m}\right\rbrack   : {\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{j}}\left( t\right)  \in  {R}_{j}}\right\}$ . For simplicity,all our algorithms are presented assuming that $\mathbf{q}$ is an acyclic join query,however in the end we extend to any general join query. A join query $\mathbf{q}$ is acyclic if there exists a tree,called join tree,such that the nodes of the tree are the relations in $\mathbf{R}$ and for every attribute $A \in  \mathbf{A}$ ,the set of nodes/relations that contain $A$ form a connected component. Let ${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathbf{q}\right)$ be the fractional edge cover of query $\mathbf{q}$ , which is a parameter that bounds the number of join results $\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$ over any database instance. More formally,for every database instance ${\mathbf{D}}^{\prime }$ with $O\left( N\right)$ tuples in each relation,it holds that $\left| {\mathbf{q}\left( {\mathbf{D}}^{\prime }\right) }\right|  = O\left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathbf{q}\right) }\right)$ as shown in [13].

基于关系聚类相关研究[20,23,43]，给定一个完全合取查询（连接查询）$\mathbf{q} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{1} \bowtie  \ldots  \bowtie  {R}_{m}$。连接查询$\mathbf{q}$在数据库实例$\mathrm{D}$上的结果集定义为$\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  = \left\{  {t \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d} \mid  \forall j \in  \left\lbrack  {1,m}\right\rbrack   : {\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{j}}\left( t\right)  \in  {R}_{j}}\right\}$。为简化起见，所有算法均假设$\mathbf{q}$为无环连接查询，但最终可推广至任意通用连接查询。当存在称为连接树的树结构，其节点为$\mathbf{R}$中的关系且对每个属性$A \in  \mathbf{A}$，包含$A$的节点/关系集形成连通分量时，连接查询$\mathbf{q}$即为无环。设${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathbf{q}\right)$为查询$\mathbf{q}$的分数边覆盖，该参数限定任意数据库实例上连接结果数$\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$的上界。更形式化地，对于每个关系包含$O\left( N\right)$元组的数据库实例${\mathbf{D}}^{\prime }$，如[13]所示，恒有$\left| {\mathbf{q}\left( {\mathbf{D}}^{\prime }\right) }\right|  = O\left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathbf{q}\right) }\right)$。

We use the notation $\operatorname{fhw}\left( \mathbf{q}\right)$ to denote the fractional hypertree width [28] of the query $\mathbf{q}$ . The fractional hypertree width roughly measures how close $\mathbf{q}$ is to being acyclic. For every acyclic join query $\mathbf{q}$ ,we have $\operatorname{fhw}\left( \mathbf{q}\right)  = 1$ . Given a cyclic join query $\mathbf{q}$ ,we convert it to an equivalent acyclic query such that each relation is the result of a (possibly cyclic) join query with fractional edge cover at most fhw(q). Hence,a cyclic join query over a database instance with $O\left( N\right)$ tuples per relation can be converted,in $O\left( {N}^{\operatorname{fhw}\left( \mathbf{q}\right) }\right)$ time,to an equivalent acyclic join query over a database instance with $O\left( {N}^{\mathrm{{fhw}}\left( \mathbf{q}\right) }\right)$ tuples per relation [13]. A more formal definition of fhw is given in Appendix E. If $\mathbf{q}$ is clear from the context,we write fhw instead of fhw(q).

采用记号$\operatorname{fhw}\left( \mathbf{q}\right)$表示查询$\mathbf{q}$的分数超树宽度[28]。该参数大致衡量$\mathbf{q}$接近无环的程度。对任意无环连接查询$\mathbf{q}$，均有$\operatorname{fhw}\left( \mathbf{q}\right)  = 1$。给定环状连接查询$\mathbf{q}$，可将其转换为等价无环查询，其中每个关系是分数边覆盖不超过fhw(q)的（可能环状）连接查询结果。因此，对每个关系含$O\left( N\right)$元组的数据库实例，环状连接查询可在$O\left( {N}^{\operatorname{fhw}\left( \mathbf{q}\right) }\right)$时间内转换为等价无环查询，其数据库实例每个关系含$O\left( {N}^{\mathrm{{fhw}}\left( \mathbf{q}\right) }\right)$元组[13]。附录E给出fhw的更形式化定义。若上下文明确，简记fhw代替fhw(q)。

For two tuples/points ${}^{1}p,q \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ let $\phi \left( {p,q}\right)  = \left| \right| p - q\left| \right|  = {\left( \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j = 1,\ldots ,d}}{\left( {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( p\right)  - {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( q\right) \right) }^{2}\right) }^{1/2}$ be the Euclidean distance between $p$ and $q$ . Throughout the paper,we use the Euclidean distance to measure the error of the clustering.

对于两个元组/点${}^{1}p,q \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，设$\phi \left( {p,q}\right)  = \left| \right| p - q\left| \right|  = {\left( \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j = 1,\ldots ,d}}{\left( {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( p\right)  - {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( q\right) \right) }^{2}\right) }^{1/2}$为$p$与$q$间的欧氏距离。全文采用欧氏距离衡量聚类误差。

Clustering. In this paper,we focus on $k$ -median and $k$ -means clustering. We start with some useful general definitions. Let $P$ be a set of points in ${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ and let $C$ be a set of $k$ centers/points in ${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ . Let $w : {\mathbb{R}}^{d} \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ > 0}$ be a weight function such that $w\left( p\right)$ is the weight of point $p \in  P$ . For a point $p \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,let $\phi \left( {p,C}\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{c \in  C}}\phi \left( {p,c}\right)$ . We define

聚类。本文聚焦于$k$-中位数聚类与$k$-均值聚类。首先给出若干通用定义：设$P$为${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$空间中的点集，$C$为${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$空间中的$k$个中心点/点集。定义权重函数$w : {\mathbb{R}}^{d} \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ > 0}$使得$w\left( p\right)$表示点$p \in  P$的权重。对于点$p \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，令$\phi \left( {p,C}\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{c \in  C}}\phi \left( {p,c}\right)$。我们定义

$$
{\mathbf{v}}_{C}\left( P\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  P}}w\left( p\right) \phi \left( {p,C}\right) ,\;\text{ and }\;{\mu }_{C}\left( P\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  P}}w\left( p\right) {\phi }^{2}\left( {p,C}\right) .
$$

If $P$ is an unweighted set,then $w\left( p\right)  = 1$ for every $p \in  P$ .

若$P$为未加权集，则对任意$p \in  P$都有$w\left( p\right)  = 1$。

$k$ -median clustering: Given a weight function $w$ ,a set of points $P$ in ${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ and a parameter $k$ , the goal is to find a set $C \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ with $\left| C\right|  = k$ such that ${\mathbf{v}}_{C}\left( P\right)$ is minimized. This is also called the geometric $k$ -median clustering problem. Equivalently,we define the discrete $k$ -median clustering problem,where the goal is to find a set $C \subseteq  P$ with $\left| C\right|  = k$ such that ${\mathbf{v}}_{C}\left( P\right)$ is minimized. Let $\operatorname{OPT}\left( P\right)  = \arg \mathop{\min }\limits_{{S \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d},\left| S\right|  = k}}{\mathbf{v}}_{S}\left( P\right)$ be a set of $k$ centers in ${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ with the minimum ${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( P\right) }\left( P\right)$ . For the discrete $k$ -median problem let ${\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}\left( P\right)  = \arg \mathop{\min }\limits_{{S \subseteq  P,\left| S\right|  = k}}{\mathbf{v}}_{S}\left( P\right)$ . It is always true that ${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( P\right) }\left( P\right)  \leq  {\mathbf{v}}_{{\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}\left( P\right) }\left( P\right)  \leq  2{\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( P\right) }\left( P\right)$ . Let GkMedian ${\mathrm{{Alg}}}_{\gamma }$ (resp. DkMedian ${\mathrm{{Alg}}}_{\gamma }$ ) be a (known) $\gamma$ -approximation algorithm for the geometric $k$ -median problem (resp. discrete $k$ - median problem) in the standard computational setting ${}^{2}$ that runs in ${T}_{\gamma }^{\text{med }}\left( \left| P\right| \right)$ time,where $\gamma$ is a constant.

$k$-中位数聚类：给定权重函数$w$、${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$空间中的点集$P$及参数$k$，目标是找到满足$\left| C\right|  = k$的集合$C \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$使${\mathbf{v}}_{C}\left( P\right)$最小化。该问题称为几何$k$-中位数聚类问题。类似地，我们定义离散$k$-中位数聚类问题，其目标是找到满足$\left| C\right|  = k$的集合$C \subseteq  P$使${\mathbf{v}}_{C}\left( P\right)$最小化。设$\operatorname{OPT}\left( P\right)  = \arg \mathop{\min }\limits_{{S \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d},\left| S\right|  = k}}{\mathbf{v}}_{S}\left( P\right)$为${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$空间中具有最小${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( P\right) }\left( P\right)$的$k$个中心点集。对于离散$k$-中位数问题，令${\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}\left( P\right)  = \arg \mathop{\min }\limits_{{S \subseteq  P,\left| S\right|  = k}}{\mathbf{v}}_{S}\left( P\right)$。恒有${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( P\right) }\left( P\right)  \leq  {\mathbf{v}}_{{\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}\left( P\right) }\left( P\right)  \leq  2{\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( P\right) }\left( P\right)$关系成立。设GkMedian${\mathrm{{Alg}}}_{\gamma }$（对应地DkMedian${\mathrm{{Alg}}}_{\gamma }$）为标准计算设定${}^{2}$下，运行时间为${T}_{\gamma }^{\text{med }}\left( \left| P\right| \right)$的几何$k$-中位数问题（对应地离散$k$-中位数问题）的（已知）$\gamma$-近似算法，其中$\gamma$为常数。

$k$ -means clustering: Given a weight function $w$ ,a set of points $P$ in ${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ and a parameter $k$ ,the goal is to find a set $C \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ with $\left| C\right|  = k$ such that ${\mu }_{C}\left( P\right)$ is minimized. This is also called the geometric $k$ -means clustering problem. Equivalently,we define the discrete $k$ -means clustering problem,where the goal is to find a set $C \subseteq  P$ with $\left| C\right|  = k$ such that ${\mu }_{C}\left( P\right)$ is minimized. Let $\operatorname{OPT}\left( P\right)  = \arg \mathop{\min }\limits_{{S \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d},\left| S\right|  = k}}{\mu }_{S}\left( P\right)$ be a set of $k$ centers in ${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ with the minimum ${\mu }_{\operatorname{OPT}\left( P\right) }\left( P\right)$ . For the discrete $k$ -means problem let ${\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}\left( P\right)  = \arg \mathop{\min }\limits_{{S \subseteq  P,\left| S\right|  = k}}{\mu }_{S}\left( P\right)$ . It is always true that ${\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( P\right) }\left( P\right)  \leq  {\mu }_{{\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}\left( P\right) }\left( P\right)  \leq  4{\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( P\right) }\left( P\right)$ . Let ${\mathrm{{GkMeansAlg}}}_{\gamma }$ (resp. DkMeansAlg ${}_{\gamma }$ ) be a (known) $\gamma$ -approximation algorithm for the geometric $k$ -means problem (resp. discrete $k$ -means problem) in the standard computational setting that runs in ${T}_{\gamma }^{\text{mean }}\left( \left| P\right| \right)$ time,where $\gamma$ is a constant.

$k$-均值聚类：给定权重函数$w$、${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$空间中的点集$P$及参数$k$，目标是找到满足$\left| C\right|  = k$的集合$C \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$使得${\mu }_{C}\left( P\right)$最小化。该问题也称为几何$k$-均值聚类问题。等价地，我们定义离散$k$-均值聚类问题，其目标是找到满足$\left| C\right|  = k$的集合$C \subseteq  P$使得${\mu }_{C}\left( P\right)$最小化。设$\operatorname{OPT}\left( P\right)  = \arg \mathop{\min }\limits_{{S \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d},\left| S\right|  = k}}{\mu }_{S}\left( P\right)$为${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$空间中具有最小${\mu }_{\operatorname{OPT}\left( P\right) }\left( P\right)$的$k$个中心点集。对于离散$k$-均值问题，令${\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}\left( P\right)  = \arg \mathop{\min }\limits_{{S \subseteq  P,\left| S\right|  = k}}{\mu }_{S}\left( P\right)$始终成立。设${\mathrm{{GkMeansAlg}}}_{\gamma }$（或DkMeansAlg${}_{\gamma }$）为标准计算环境下运行时间为${T}_{\gamma }^{\text{mean }}\left( \left| P\right| \right)$的几何$k$-均值问题（或离散$k$-均值问题）的（已知）$\gamma$近似算法，其中$\gamma$为常数。

For simplicity,we use the same notation ${T}_{Y}^{\text{med }}\left( \cdot \right)$ for the running time of GkMedianAlg and DkMedianAlg. Similarly,we use the same notation ${T}_{Y}^{\text{mean }}\left( \cdot \right)$ for the running time of GkMeansAlg and DkMeansAlg. We also use the same notation OPT(-) for the optimum solution for $k$ -means and $k$ -median clustering problems. It is always clear from the context whether we are referring to $k$ -means or $k$ -median clustering.

为简化表述，我们使用相同符号${T}_{Y}^{\text{med }}\left( \cdot \right)$表示GkMedianAlg与DkMedianAlg的运行时间，同理使用${T}_{Y}^{\text{mean }}\left( \cdot \right)$表示GkMeansAlg与DkMeansAlg的运行时间。对于$k$-均值与$k$-中位数聚类问题的最优解，我们统一采用OPT(-)表示。根据上下文可明确区分所指的是$k$-均值还是$k$-中位数聚类。

In this paper,we study (both geometric and discrete) $k$ -median and $k$ -means clustering on the result of a join query:

本文研究连接查询结果上的（几何与离散）$k$-中位数与$k$-均值聚类问题：

Definition 1.1. [Relational $k$ -median clustering] Given a database instance D,a join query $\mathbf{q}$ ,and a positive integer parameter $k$ ,the goal is to find a set $\mathcal{S} \subseteq  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ of size $\left| \mathcal{S}\right|  = k$ such that ${\mathbf{v}}_{C}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ is minimized. In the discrete relational $k$ -median clustering the set $\mathcal{S}$ should be a subset of $\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ .

定义1.1 [关系型$k$-中位数聚类] 给定数据库实例D、连接查询$\mathbf{q}$及正整数参数$k$，目标是找到大小为$\left| \mathcal{S}\right|  = k$的集合$\mathcal{S} \subseteq  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$使得${\mathbf{v}}_{C}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$最小化。在离散关系型$k$-中位数聚类中，集合$\mathcal{S}$应为$\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$的子集。

Definition 1.2. [Relational $k$ -means clustering] Given a database instance $\mathbf{D}$ ,a join query $\mathbf{q}$ ,and a positive integer parameter $k$ ,the goal is to find a set $\mathcal{S} \subseteq  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ of size $\left| \mathcal{S}\right|  = k$ such that the ${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ is minimized. In the discrete relational $k$ -means clustering the set $\mathcal{S}$ should be a subset of $\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$ .

定义1.2 [关系型$k$-均值聚类] 给定数据库实例$\mathbf{D}$、连接查询$\mathbf{q}$和正整数参数$k$，目标是找到大小为$\left| \mathcal{S}\right|  = k$的集合$\mathcal{S} \subseteq  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$使得${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$最小化。在离散关系型$k$-均值聚类中，集合$\mathcal{S}$应为$\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$的子集。

---

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{1}$ The terms points and tuples are used interchangeably.

${}^{1}$ 术语"点"与"元组"可互换使用。

${}^{2}$ In the standard computational setting data is stored in one table.

${}^{2}$ 标准计算设定中数据存储于单一表内。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

By relational $k$ -median or $k$ -means clustering,we are referring to the geometric versions,unless explicitly stated otherwise for the discrete variants. We propose results for both versions. We note that the clustering problem on relational data is defined over the unweighted set $\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ . In order to propose efficient algorithms we will need to construct weighted subset of tuples (coresets) that are used to derive small approximation factors for the relational clustering problems.

本文所述关系型$k$-中位数或$k$-均值聚类均指几何版本，除非特别说明为离散变体。我们针对两种版本提出研究结果。需注意关系数据上的聚类问题定义在未加权集合$\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$上。为设计高效算法，我们需要构建元组的加权子集(核心集)，用于推导关系型聚类问题的较小近似因子。

Approximation. We say that an algorithm is a relative $\beta$ -approximation algorithm for the relational $k$ -median clustering if it returns a set $\mathcal{S}$ of size $k$ such that ${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \beta  \cdot  {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ . Next,we say that an algorithm is an additive $\beta$ -approximation algorithm for the relational $k$ - median clustering if it returns a set $\mathcal{S}$ of size $k$ such that ${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  + \beta$ . In this paper,we focus on relative approximation algorithms,so when we refer to a $\beta$ -approximation algorithm we always mean relative $\beta$ -approximation. Equivalently,we define relative and additive approximation algorithms for the relational $k$ -means clustering.

近似算法。若算法返回大小为$k$的集合$\mathcal{S}$且满足${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \beta  \cdot  {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$，则称其为关系型$k$-中位数聚类的相对$\beta$-近似算法；若满足${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  + \beta$，则称为加性$\beta$-近似算法。本文聚焦相对近似算法，故$\beta$-近似算法特指相对近似。同理可定义关系型$k$-均值聚类的相对与加性近似算法。

### 1.2 Related work

### 1.2 相关工作

There is a lot of research on $k$ -median and $k$ -means clustering in the standard computational setting. For the $k$ -median clustering there are several polynomial time algorithms with constant approximation ratio $\left\lbrack  {{12},{19},{39}}\right\rbrack$ . For the $k$ -means clustering,there are also several constant approximation algorithms such as [34]. In practice, a local search algorithm [40] is mostly used with a $O\left( {\log k}\right)$ approximation ratio. Furthermore,coresets (formal definition in Section 2) have been used to design efficient clustering algorithms $\left\lbrack  {{14},{31},{32}}\right\rbrack$ . Coresets are also used to propose efficient clustering algorithms in the streaming setting [16, 29] or the MPC model [15, 26]. All these algorithms work in the standard computational setting and it is not clear how to efficiently extend them to relational clustering.

标准计算设定下已有大量$k$-中位数与$k$-均值聚类研究。$k$-中位数聚类存在多个具有常数近似比$\left\lbrack  {{12},{19},{39}}\right\rbrack$的多项式时间算法。$k$-均值聚类也有若干常数近似算法如[34]。实践中多采用近似比为$O\left( {\log k}\right)$的局部搜索算法[40]。此外，核心集(第2章正规定义)已被用于设计高效聚类算法$\left\lbrack  {{14},{31},{32}}\right\rbrack$，也应用于流计算[16,29]和MPC模型[15,26]。这些算法均基于标准计算设定，如何扩展至关系型聚类尚不明确。

In its most general form, relational clustering is referring to clustering objects connected by links representing persistent relationships between them. Different variations of relational clustering have been studied over the years in the database and data mining community, for example [9, 17, ${27},{33},{37},{41},{44}\rbrack$ . However,papers in this line of work either do not handle clustering on join results or their methods do not have theoretical guarantees.

广义而言，关系型聚类指对通过持久关系链接相连的对象进行聚类。数据库与数据挖掘领域已研究多种变体，如[9,17,${27},{33},{37},{41},{44}\rbrack$]。但现有研究要么未处理连接结果的聚类问题，要么缺乏理论保证。

The discrete relational $k$ -means clustering problem has been recently studied in the literature. Khamis et al. [35],gave an efficient implementation of the Lloyd’s $k$ -means heuristic in the relational setting, however it is known that the algorithm terminates in a local minimum without any guarantee on the approximation factor. Relative approximation algorithms are also known for the relational $k$ -means clustering problem. Curtin et al. [23] construct a weighted set of tuples (grid-coreset) such that an approximation algorithm for the weighted $k$ -means clustering in the grid-coreset returns an approximation solution to the relational $k$ -means clustering. Their algorithm runs in $\widetilde{O}\left( {{k}^{m}{N}^{\text{fhw }} + {T}_{Y}^{\text{mean }}\left( {k}^{m}\right) }\right)$ time and has a $\left( {{\gamma }^{2} + {4\gamma }\sqrt{\gamma } + {4\gamma }}\right)$ -approximation factor. For some join queries,the approximation factor can be improved to $\left( {{4\gamma } + 2\sqrt{\gamma } + 1}\right)$ . Moseley et al. [43] designed a relational implementation of the $k$ -means++ algorithm [8,11] to derive a better weighted coreset. For a constant $\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,their algorithm runs in $O\left( {{k}^{4}{N}^{\text{fhw }}\log N + {k}^{2}{N}^{\text{fhw }}{\log }^{9}N + {T}_{Y}^{\text{mean }}\left( {k\log N}\right) }\right)$ expected time and has a $\left( {{320} + {644}\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma }\right)$ -approximation factor. No efficient relative approximation algorithm is known for the relational $k$ -median problem.

离散关系型$k$-均值聚类问题近年已在文献中被广泛研究。Khamis等人[35]提出了Lloyd$k$-均值启发式算法在关系型场景中的高效实现，但该算法会终止于局部最优解，且无法保证近似因子精度。针对关系型$k$-均值聚类问题，相对近似算法亦有相关研究。Curtin等人[23]构建了加权元组集合（网格核心集），使得该核心集上的加权$k$-均值聚类近似算法可转化为关系型$k$-均值聚类的近似解。其算法时间复杂度为$\widetilde{O}\left( {{k}^{m}{N}^{\text{fhw }} + {T}_{Y}^{\text{mean }}\left( {k}^{m}\right) }\right)$，并具有$\left( {{\gamma }^{2} + {4\gamma }\sqrt{\gamma } + {4\gamma }}\right)$-近似因子。对于某些连接查询，近似因子可优化至$\left( {{4\gamma } + 2\sqrt{\gamma } + 1}\right)$。Moseley等人[43]设计了$k$-means++算法[8,11]的关系型实现以获得更优加权核心集。当$\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$为常数时，其算法期望时间复杂度为$O\left( {{k}^{4}{N}^{\text{fhw }}\log N + {k}^{2}{N}^{\text{fhw }}{\log }^{9}N + {T}_{Y}^{\text{mean }}\left( {k\log N}\right) }\right)$，近似因子为$\left( {{320} + {644}\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma }\right)$。目前关系型$k$-中位数问题尚未发现高效的相对近似算法。

Additive approximation algorithms are also known for relational clustering problems. Chen et al. [20], constructed coresets for empirical risk minimization problems in relational data. Their algorithm is quite general and can support relational clustering,i.e., $k$ -median and $k$ -means can be formulated as risk minimization problems. They work independently in every relation to compute a good enough coreset and then by the aggregation tree algorithm they merge the solutions carefully using properties of the $k$ -center clustering. We note that the authors do not design algorithms for relational clustering problems, instead, they compute a coreset such that any approximation algorithm (for $k$ -median or $k$ -means) on the coreset returns an (additive) approximation of the relational $k$ -median or $k$ -means problem. Running GkMedianAlg on top of their coreset leads to a randomized algorithm that runs in $O\left( {{N}^{\text{fhw }} + {T}_{Y}^{\text{med }}\left( 1\right) }\right)$ time and has an $\varepsilon  \cdot  \gamma  \cdot  \operatorname{diam}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ additive approximation term,with high probability,where $\operatorname{diam}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ is the largest Euclidean distance between two tuples in $\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ . For the relational $k$ -means clustering,their algorithm runs in $O\left( {{N}^{\text{fhw }} + {T}_{Y}^{\text{mean }}\left( 1\right) }\right)$ time,and has an $\varepsilon  \cdot  \gamma  \cdot  {\operatorname{diam}}^{2}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ additive approximation term,with high probability. In all cases we assume that $\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ is a small constant.

加性近似算法在关系型聚类问题中亦有应用。Chen等人[20]针对关系数据中的经验风险最小化问题构建了核心集。该算法具有普适性，可支持关系型聚类——即将$k$-中位数与$k$-均值问题表述为风险最小化问题。算法在各关系上独立计算足够优质的核心集后，通过聚合树算法结合$k$-中心聚类的特性谨慎合并解。需注意的是，作者并未直接设计关系型聚类算法，而是通过核心集使得任何针对$k$-中位数或$k$-均值的近似算法都能返回关系型问题的（加性）近似解。在其核心集上运行GkMedianAlg可得到时间复杂度$O\left( {{N}^{\text{fhw }} + {T}_{Y}^{\text{med }}\left( 1\right) }\right)$、高概率具有$\varepsilon  \cdot  \gamma  \cdot  \operatorname{diam}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$加性近似项的随机算法，其中$\operatorname{diam}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$表示$\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$中两元组间最大欧氏距离。对于关系型$k$-均值聚类，该算法时间复杂度为$O\left( {{N}^{\text{fhw }} + {T}_{Y}^{\text{mean }}\left( 1\right) }\right)$，高概率具有$\varepsilon  \cdot  \gamma  \cdot  {\operatorname{diam}}^{2}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$加性近似项。所有分析均假设$\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$为较小常数。

Table 1. Comparison of our new algorithms with the state-of-the-art relative approximation algorithms. For our new algorithms we show the approximation for the discrete relational $k$ -median and $k$ -means clustering problems. For the geometric version of the studied problems the approximation factor of our algorithms is always $\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$ . The running time is shown in data complexity. We assume that $\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ is a small constant. The notation $\widetilde{O}$ is used to hide ${\log }^{O\left( 1\right) }N$ factors from the running time. ${T}_{Y}^{\text{med }}\left( y\right)$ (resp. ${T}_{Y}^{\text{mean }}\left( y\right)$ ) is the running time of a known $\gamma$ -approximation algorithm over $y$ points for the $k$ -median (resp. $k$ -means) clustering in the standard computational setting. fhw is the fractional hypertree width of $q$ . The number of attributes in the query $q$ is denoted by $d$ . The letter $\mathrm{R}$ stands for randomized,while $\mathrm{D}$ stands for deterministic algorithm.

表1. 我们新算法与当前最优相对近似算法的对比。针对离散关系型$k$-中位数和$k$-均值聚类问题，展示了新算法的近似效果。在几何版本的研究问题中，我们的算法近似因子恒为$\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$。运行时间以数据复杂度表示。假设$\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$为较小常数。符号$\widetilde{O}$用于隐藏运行时间中的${\log }^{O\left( 1\right) }N$因子。${T}_{Y}^{\text{med }}\left( y\right)$（相应地${T}_{Y}^{\text{mean }}\left( y\right)$）表示标准计算环境下，已知$\gamma$-近似算法在$y$个点上求解$k$-中位数（相应地$k$-均值）聚类的运行时间。fhw表示$q$的分数超树宽度。查询$q$中的属性数量记为$d$。字母$\mathrm{R}$代表随机化算法，而$\mathrm{D}$代表确定性算法。

<!-- Media -->

Finally, there is a lot of recent work on relational algorithms for learning problems such as, linear regression and factorization $\left\lbrack  {{36},{38},{47},{50}}\right\rbrack$ ,SVMs $\left\lbrack  {3,4,{54}}\right\rbrack$ ,Independent Gaussian Mixture models [21, 22]. In [49] the authors give a nice survey about learning over relational data. Generally, in databases there is an interesting line of work solving combinatorial problems over relational data without first computing the join results, such as ranked enumeration [24, 25, 52], quantiles [53], direct access [18],diversity [6,10,42],and top- $k$ [52].

最后，近期涌现了大量针对线性回归、因子分解$\left\lbrack  {{36},{38},{47},{50}}\right\rbrack$、支持向量机$\left\lbrack  {3,4,{54}}\right\rbrack$、独立高斯混合模型[21,22]等学习问题的关系型算法研究。文献[49]对关系数据学习进行了出色综述。数据库领域存在一条有趣的研究路线，可不预先计算连接结果就能解决关系数据上的组合问题，例如排序枚举[24,25,52]、分位数[53]、直接访问[18]、多样性[6,10,42]以及top-$k$查询[52]。

### 1.3 Our results

### 1.3 研究成果

The main results for the discrete relational clustering in this paper are summarized in Table 1. In all cases,we assume that $\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ is a small constant decided by the user.

本文关于离散关系型聚类的主要成果总结于表1。所有情况下，我们假设$\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$是由用户决定的小常数。

First,we present a deterministic $\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$ -approximation algorithm for the (geometric) relational $k$ -median clustering that runs in $O\left( {{k}^{{2d} + 2}{N}^{\text{fhw }}{\log }^{d + 2}N + {T}_{\gamma }^{\text{med }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$ time. Then,we show a randomized $\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$ -approximation algorithm for the (geometric) relational $k$ -median clustering that works with high probability and runs in $O\left( {{k}^{2}{N}^{\text{fhw }}\log N + {k}^{4}{\log }^{3}\left( N\right) {\log }^{d}\left( k\right)  + {T}_{\gamma }^{\text{med }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$ time. If ${k}^{2}\log \left( N\right)  < {\log }^{d}\left( k\right)$ the running time can be improved to $O\left( {{k}^{2}{N}^{\text{fhw }}\log N + {k}^{6}{\log }^{4}\left( N\right)  + }\right.$ $\left. {{T}_{\gamma }^{\text{med }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$ . In the discrete case,the approximation factor is $\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$ . These are the first known efficient relative approximation algorithms for the relational $k$ -median clustering problem.

首先，我们提出确定性$\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$-近似算法用于（几何）关系型$k$-中位数聚类，其运行时间为$O\left( {{k}^{{2d} + 2}{N}^{\text{fhw }}{\log }^{d + 2}N + {T}_{\gamma }^{\text{med }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$。随后给出随机化$\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$-近似算法，该算法以高概率适用于（几何）关系型$k$-中位数聚类，运行时间为$O\left( {{k}^{2}{N}^{\text{fhw }}\log N + {k}^{4}{\log }^{3}\left( N\right) {\log }^{d}\left( k\right)  + {T}_{\gamma }^{\text{med }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$。若${k}^{2}\log \left( N\right)  < {\log }^{d}\left( k\right)$，运行时间可优化至$O\left( {{k}^{2}{N}^{\text{fhw }}\log N + {k}^{6}{\log }^{4}\left( N\right)  + }\right.$$\left. {{T}_{\gamma }^{\text{med }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$。离散情形下近似因子为$\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$。这些是首个针对关系型$k$-中位数聚类问题的高效相对近似算法。

We extend our methods to show algorithms with the same guarantees for the relational $k$ - means clustering. We give a deterministic $\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$ -approximation algorithm for the (geometric) relational $k$ -means clustering that runs in $O\left( {{k}^{{2d} + 2}{N}^{\text{fhw }}{\log }^{d + 2}N + {T}_{Y}^{\text{mean }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$ time. Furthermore,we give a randomized $\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$ -approximation algorithm that runs in $O\left( {{k}^{2}{N}^{\text{fhw }}\log N + }\right.$ $\left. {{k}^{4}{\log }^{3}\left( N\right) {\log }^{d}\left( k\right)  + {T}_{\gamma }^{\text{mean }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$ time. If ${k}^{2}\log \left( N\right)  < {\log }^{d}\left( k\right)$ the running time can be improved to $O\left( {{k}^{2}{N}^{\text{fhw }}\log N + {k}^{6}{\log }^{4}\left( N\right)  + {T}_{Y}^{\text{mean }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$ . In the discrete case the approximation factor is $\left( {4 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$ . Our algorithms significantly improve both the approximation factor and the running time of the known algorithms on relational $k$ -means clustering. Due to space limit,in the next sections we focus on the relational $k$ -median clustering. We show all the results for the $k$ -means clustering in Appendix A.

我们将方法扩展至关系型$k$-均值聚类，提出具有相同保障的算法。针对（几何）关系型$k$-均值聚类，我们给出一个确定性$\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$-近似算法，其时间复杂度为$O\left( {{k}^{{2d} + 2}{N}^{\text{fhw }}{\log }^{d + 2}N + {T}_{Y}^{\text{mean }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$。此外，我们还提出一个随机化$\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$-近似算法，运行时间为$O\left( {{k}^{2}{N}^{\text{fhw }}\log N + }\right.$$\left. {{k}^{4}{\log }^{3}\left( N\right) {\log }^{d}\left( k\right)  + {T}_{\gamma }^{\text{mean }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$。若${k}^{2}\log \left( N\right)  < {\log }^{d}\left( k\right)$，运行时间可优化至$O\left( {{k}^{2}{N}^{\text{fhw }}\log N + {k}^{6}{\log }^{4}\left( N\right)  + {T}_{Y}^{\text{mean }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$。在离散情况下，近似因子为$\left( {4 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$。我们的算法在关系型$k$-均值聚类上显著提升了现有算法的近似因子和运行时间。由于篇幅限制，后续章节将聚焦关系型$k$-中位数聚类，所有$k$-均值聚类的实验结果详见附录A。

Remark 1. While our algorithms work for both acyclic and cyclic join queries, we first present all our results assuming that $q$ is an acyclic join query. In Section 4.2 we extend our results for every join query using the generalized hypetree decomposition [28]. From now on we consider that $\mathbf{q}$ is an acyclic join query,so fhw $= 1$ .

注1. 虽然算法适用于无环和有环连接查询，但本文首先基于$q$为无环连接查询的假设展开所有结论。在第4.2节中，我们利用广义超树分解[28]将结果推广至所有连接查询。下文默认$\mathbf{q}$为无环连接查询，故fhw$= 1$。

Remark 2. For the relational $k$ -means clustering,the algorithm in [43] is generally faster than the other algorithms for $k = O\left( 1\right)$ ,by $\log N$ factors.

注2. 对于关系型$k$-均值聚类，文献[43]提出的算法通常比其他算法快$\log N$倍，尤其当$k = O\left( 1\right)$时。

## 2 PRELIMINARIES

## 2 基础知识

### 2.1 Coreset

### 2.1 核心集

We give the definition of coresets for both the relational $k$ -median and $k$ -means clustering problems. Our algorithms construct small enough coresets to approximate the cost of the relational clustering.

我们给出关系型$k$-中位数与$k$-均值聚类问题的核心集定义。所提算法通过构建足够小的核心集来近似关系型聚类成本。

A weighted set $\mathcal{C} \subseteq  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ is an $\varepsilon$ -coreset for the relational $k$ -median clustering problem on $\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ if for any set of $k$ centers $Y \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,

加权集$\mathcal{C} \subseteq  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$称为$\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$上关系型$k$-中位数聚类问题的$\varepsilon$-核心集，当且仅当对于任意$k$中心点集$Y \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，

$$
\left( {1 - \varepsilon }\right) {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( C\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) . \tag{1}
$$

Similarly,a weighted set $\mathcal{C}$ is an $\varepsilon$ -coreset for the relational $k$ -means clustering problem,if for any set of $k$ centers $Y \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,

同理，加权集$\mathcal{C}$称为关系型$k$-均值聚类问题的$\varepsilon$-核心集，当且仅当对于任意$k$中心点集$Y \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，

$$
\left( {1 - \varepsilon }\right) {\mu }_{Y}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {\mu }_{Y}\left( \mathbf{C}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) {\mu }_{Y}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) . \tag{2}
$$

### 2.2 Data structures for aggregation queries

### 2.2 聚合查询数据结构

In this subsection, we show how we can combine known results in database theory to answer aggregation queries in linear time with respect to the size of the database.

本小节阐述如何结合数据库理论的已知成果，实现与数据库规模线性相关的聚合查询响应。

Let $\mathcal{R}$ be an axis-parallel hyper-rectangle in ${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ .The goal is i) count the number of tuples $\mid  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \cap$ $\mathcal{R} \mid$ ,and ii) sample uniformly at random from $\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{R}$ . The axis-parallel hyper-rectangle $\mathcal{R}$ is defined as the product of $d$ intervals over the attributes,i.e., $\mathcal{R} = \left\{  {{I}_{1} \times  \ldots  \times  {I}_{d}}\right)$ ,where ${I}_{i} = \left\lbrack  {{a}_{i},{b}_{i}}\right\rbrack$ for ${a}_{i},{b}_{i} \in  \mathbb{R}$ . Hence, $\mathcal{R}$ defines a set of $d$ linear inequalities over the attributes,i.e.,a tuple $t$ lies in $\mathcal{R}$ if and only if ${a}_{j} \leq  {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( t\right)  \leq  {b}_{j}$ for every ${A}_{j} \in  \mathbf{A}$ . Let $p$ be a tuple in a relation ${R}_{i}$ . If ${a}_{j} \leq  {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( p\right)  \leq  {b}_{j}$ for every ${A}_{j} \in  {\mathbf{A}}_{i}$ ,then we keep $p$ in ${R}_{i}$ . Otherwise,we remove it. The set of surviving tuples is exactly the set of tuples that might lead to join results in $\mathcal{R}$ . Let ${\mathrm{D}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathrm{D}$ be the new database instance such that $\mathbf{q}\left( {\mathrm{D}}^{\prime }\right)  = \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{R}$ . The set ${\mathrm{D}}^{\prime }$ is found in $O\left( N\right)$ time. Using Yannakakis algorithm [55] we can count $\left| {\mathbf{q}\left( {\mathbf{D}}^{\prime }\right) }\right|$ in $O\left( {N\log N}\right)$ time and using [56] we can sample $z$ tuples from $\mathbf{q}\left( {\mathrm{D}}^{\prime }\right)$ in $O\left( {\left( {N + z}\right) \log N}\right)$ time. Using hashing,we can improve the running time of the algorithms to $O\left( N\right)$ and $O\left( {N + z\log N}\right)$ ,respectively.

设$\mathcal{R}$为${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$中的轴对齐超矩形。目标为：i)统计元组$\mid  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \cap$$\mathcal{R} \mid$的数量，ii)从$\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{R}$中均匀随机采样。该轴对齐超矩形$\mathcal{R}$定义为各属性上$d$个区间的笛卡尔积，即$\mathcal{R} = \left\{  {{I}_{1} \times  \ldots  \times  {I}_{d}}\right)$，其中对每个${a}_{i},{b}_{i} \in  \mathbb{R}$有${I}_{i} = \left\lbrack  {{a}_{i},{b}_{i}}\right\rbrack$。因此$\mathcal{R}$定义了关于属性的$d$个线性不等式，即元组$t$属于$\mathcal{R}$当且仅当对每个${A}_{j} \in  \mathbf{A}$满足${a}_{j} \leq  {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( t\right)  \leq  {b}_{j}$。设$p$为关系${R}_{i}$中的元组，若对每个${A}_{j} \in  {\mathbf{A}}_{i}$都满足${a}_{j} \leq  {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( p\right)  \leq  {b}_{j}$，则保留${R}_{i}$中的$p$，否则移除。幸存元组集即为可能产生$\mathcal{R}$内连接结果的元组集合。设${\mathrm{D}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathrm{D}$为满足$\mathbf{q}\left( {\mathrm{D}}^{\prime }\right)  = \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{R}$的新数据库实例，集合${\mathrm{D}}^{\prime }$可在$O\left( N\right)$时间内找到。应用Yannakakis算法[55]可在$O\left( {N\log N}\right)$时间内统计$\left| {\mathbf{q}\left( {\mathbf{D}}^{\prime }\right) }\right|$，利用文献[56]可在$O\left( {\left( {N + z}\right) \log N}\right)$时间内从$\mathbf{q}\left( {\mathrm{D}}^{\prime }\right)$采样$z$个元组。通过哈希技术可将算法运行时间分别提升至$O\left( N\right)$和$O\left( {N + z\log N}\right)$。

LEMMA 2.1. Let $\mathcal{R}$ be a rectangle in ${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ . There exists an algorithm $\operatorname{CountRect}\left( {\mathbf{q},\mathbf{D},\mathcal{R}}\right)$ to count $\left| {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{R}}\right|$ in $O\left( {N\log N}\right)$ time or $O\left( N\right)$ time with high probability. Furtheremore,there exists an algorithm SampleRect(q,D,R,z)to sample $z$ samples from $\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{R}$ in $O\left( {\left( {N + z}\right) \log N}\right)$ time or $O\left( {N + z\log N}\right)$ time with high probability.

引理2.1. 设$\mathcal{R}$为${\mathbb{R}}^{d}$中的矩形。存在算法$\operatorname{CountRect}\left( {\mathbf{q},\mathbf{D},\mathcal{R}}\right)$能以$O\left( {N\log N}\right)$时间或高概率$O\left( N\right)$时间统计$\left| {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{R}}\right|$。此外，存在算法SampleRect(q,D,R,z)能以$O\left( {\left( {N + z}\right) \log N}\right)$时间或高概率$O\left( {N + z\log N}\right)$时间从$\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{R}$中采样$z$个样本。

Let $B \subseteq  \mathbf{A}$ be a subset of the attributes. The result in Lemma 2.1 can also be used (straightforwardly) to compute $\left| {{\bar{\pi }}_{B}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \cap  \mathcal{R}}\right|$ or sample $z$ samples from ${\bar{\pi }}_{B}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \cap  \mathcal{R}$ in the same running time,since $\left| {{\bar{\pi }}_{B}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \cap  \mathcal{R}}\right|  = \left| {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{R}}\right|$ .

设$B \subseteq  \mathbf{A}$为属性的一个子集。由于$\left| {{\bar{\pi }}_{B}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \cap  \mathcal{R}}\right|  = \left| {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{R}}\right|$，引理2.1的结果也可直接用于在相同运行时间内计算$\left| {{\bar{\pi }}_{B}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \cap  \mathcal{R}}\right|$或从${\bar{\pi }}_{B}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \cap  \mathcal{R}$中采样$z$个样本。

### 2.3 High level ideas

### 2.3 核心思路

Before we start with the technical sections of the paper, we give high level ideas of our methods. In Section 3,we assume that a set of centers $X$ for the relational $k$ -median clustering is given such that ${\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ is within a constant factor from the optimum $k$ -median error with $\left| X\right|  > k$ . Using $X$ ,we construct a coreset $C$ for the relational $k$ -median problem. Then we run ${\mathrm{{GkMedianAlg}}}_{v}$ (or ${\mathrm{{DkMeansAlg}}}_{\gamma }$ for the discrete version) on $\mathcal{C}$ to obtain the final solution $\mathcal{S}$ . While all previous papers on relational clustering $\left\lbrack  {{20},{23},{43}}\right\rbrack$ construct weighted coresets,the coresets that were used are sub-optimal. Instead, we use and modify the coreset construction from [32], which is a more optimized coreset for the $k$ -median (and $k$ -means) clustering under the Euclidean metric,in the standard computational setting. Of course, using such a coreset comes at a cost. First, it is not clear how to construct a set $X$ with the desired properties on relational data,efficiently. In Section 4 we show how to construct $X$ designing a hierarchical method over the attributes A. Second,it is not straightforward to construct the coreset in [32] on relational data,given $X$ . In fact,the coreset construction in [32] cannot be applied (efficiently) in the relational setting. Hence, inspired by [32],we design a novel small coreset based on $X$ and show that it can be applied to relational data.

在进入论文技术章节前，我们首先概述方法的核心思想。第3节中，我们假设已给定关系型$k$-中位数聚类的一组中心点$X$，使得${\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$与最优$k$-中位数误差相差常数倍（给定$\left| X\right|  > k$）。基于$X$，我们为关系型$k$-中位数问题构建核心集$C$，随后在$\mathcal{C}$上运行${\mathrm{{GkMedianAlg}}}_{v}$（离散版本则使用${\mathrm{{DkMeansAlg}}}_{\gamma }$）以获得最终解$\mathcal{S}$。尽管以往所有关系型聚类$\left\lbrack  {{20},{23},{43}}\right\rbrack$研究都构建加权核心集，但所用核心集均非最优。我们采用并改进文献[32]的核心集构建方法——该方法是欧氏度量下标准计算环境中针对$k$-中位数（及$k$-均值）聚类的更优核心集。当然，使用此类核心集需付出代价：首先，如何高效地在关系数据上构建满足条件的集合$X$尚不明确；第4节我们将展示如何通过对属性A设计分层方法来构建$X$。其次，在给定$X$的情况下，将[32]的核心集构建方法应用于关系数据并非易事。实际上，[32]的构建方法无法（高效）适用于关系型场景。因此，受[32]启发，我们基于$X$设计了一种新型小型核心集，并证明其可应用于关系数据。

More specifically,for any center ${x}_{i} \in  X$ ,in [32],the authors first compute all points in the dataset that have ${x}_{i}$ as the closest center in $X$ . Let ${P}_{i}$ be this set of points. Then,they construct a grid around ${x}_{i}$ ,and from every cell $\square$ in the grid,they add one representative point from ${P}_{i} \cap  \square$ with weight $\left| {{P}_{i} \cap  \square }\right|$ . Unfortunately,in our setting we cannot compute ${P}_{i}$ and/or $\left| {{P}_{i} \cap  \square }\right|$ efficiently.

具体而言，对于任意中心点${x}_{i} \in  X$，文献[32]作者首先计算数据集中以${x}_{i}$为$X$内最近中心的所有点，记该点集为${P}_{i}$。随后围绕${x}_{i}$构建网格，从每个网格单元$\square$中选取${P}_{i} \cap  \square$的一个代表点并赋予权重$\left| {{P}_{i} \cap  \square }\right|$。然而在我们的设定中，无法高效计算${P}_{i}$和/或$\left| {{P}_{i} \cap  \square }\right|$。

We resolve the two issues as follows. First,we construct a grid around ${x}_{i}$ ,but instead of computing all tuples that have ${x}_{i}$ as the closest center and process all cells,we check whether a cell $\square$ is close enough to the center ${x}_{i}$ (Equation (3) in the next section). If not then we skip the cell. If yes, then we take a representative tuple from $\square$ and we set its weight to be the number of tuples in $\square$ that do not lie in a different cell that has been already processed by our algorithm. Of course, the weight of a representative tuple now is not the same as in [32] and we might count tuples even outside of ${P}_{i}$ ,however with a careful analysis we make sure that the overall error is still bounded. The second problem though,still remains; we need to count the number of tuples in $\square$ excluding the cells we have already visited from previous centers in $X$ (notice that the grid cells of two different centers in $X$ might intersect). We propose two methods to achieve it. In Section 3.1 we give a deterministic method that constructs the arrangement of the complement of the visited cells and we use Lemma 2.1 to count the number of tuples,exactly. However,this algorithm requires $\Omega \left( {{\left| X\right| }^{d}N}\right)$ time. In Section 3.2 we give a more involved and faster randomized approximation algorithm to count the number of tuples based on sampling.

我们通过以下方式解决这两个问题。首先，围绕${x}_{i}$构建网格，但并非计算所有以${x}_{i}$为最近中心的元组并处理所有单元格，而是检查单元格$\square$是否足够接近中心${x}_{i}$（见下节公式(3)）。若不符合条件则跳过该单元格。若符合条件，则从$\square$中选取代表元组，并将其权重设为该单元格中未被算法已处理其他单元格包含的元组数量。需注意，此时代表元组的权重与文献[32]不同，且可能统计到${P}_{i}$之外的元组，但通过严谨分析可确保总体误差仍受控。第二个问题依然存在：需要统计$\square$中排除$X$内已访问单元格后的元组数量（注意$X$中不同中心的网格单元格可能相交）。我们提出两种解决方案：第3.1节给出确定性方法，通过构建已访问单元格补集排列并引用引理2.1实现精确计数，但该算法需要$\Omega \left( {{\left| X\right| }^{d}N}\right)$时间；第3.2节提出更复杂快速的随机近似算法，基于抽样进行元组计数。

## 3 FROM MANY CENTERS TO EXACTLY $k$ CENTERS

## 3 从多中心到精确$k$中心

We describe an algorithm that constructs a coreset for the relational $k$ -median clustering over the (multi-set) projection of $\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ on an arbitrary subset of attributes. Let ${\mathrm{A}}_{u} \subseteq  \mathrm{A}$ be a subset of the attributes. Let ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ be the multi-set ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\bar{\pi }}_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ . Notice that $\left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right|  = \left| {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right|$ and its size can be computed in $O\left( N\right)$ time using Yannakakis algorithm. Let $n = \left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right|$ and ${d}_{u} = \left| {\mathbf{A}}_{u}\right|$ . Assume that $X$ is a set of points in ${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$ such that ${\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \alpha  \cdot  {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ ,where $\alpha  > 1$ is a constant. Notice that

我们提出一种算法，用于构建关系型$k$-中值聚类在$\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$任意属性子集投影（多重集）上的核心集。设${\mathrm{A}}_{u} \subseteq  \mathrm{A}$为属性子集，${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$为多重集${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\bar{\pi }}_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$。注意$\left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right|  = \left| {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right|$及其规模可通过Yannakakis算法在$O\left( N\right)$时间内计算得出。令$n = \left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right|$与${d}_{u} = \left| {\mathbf{A}}_{u}\right|$，假设$X$是${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$空间中的点集且满足${\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \alpha  \cdot  {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$（其中$\alpha  > 1$为常数）。需注意

$$
{\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}\phi \left( {{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) ,X}\right) .
$$

We also assume that $r$ is a real number such that ${\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq  \alpha  \cdot  {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)$ . In fact, it follows from the following sections that we can also assume $\frac{r}{\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \sqrt{2}} \leq  {\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ . Note that we do not assume anything about the size of $X$ . In the next section,we show that we can always consider $\left| X\right|  = O\left( {k}^{2}\right)$ .

我们还假设$r$是一个实数，使得${\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq  \alpha  \cdot  {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)$。实际上，从后续章节可以看出，我们也可以假设$\frac{r}{\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \sqrt{2}} \leq  {\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$。请注意，我们并未对$X$的大小作任何假设。下一节将证明，我们总能考虑$\left| X\right|  = O\left( {k}^{2}\right)$。

算法1：RELCLUSTERINGSLOW$\left( {\mathbf{q},\mathbf{D},{\mathbf{A}}_{u},X,\alpha ,r,\varepsilon }\right)$

$n = \left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right|  = \left| {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) )}\right| \;$ (using Lemma 2.1);

$n = \left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right|  = \left| {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) )}\right| \;$（使用引理2.1）；

$\Phi  = \frac{r}{\alpha n};\;G = \varnothing ;\;C = \varnothing ;$

	foreach $j = 0,1,\ldots ,2\log \left( {\alpha n}\right)$ do

		${Q}_{i,j} \leftarrow$以${x}_{i}$为中心、边长为${2}^{j}\Phi$的轴平行正方形；

		${V}_{i,j} = {Q}_{i,j} \smallsetminus  {Q}_{i,j - 1};\;{\bar{V}}_{i,j} \leftarrow$ grid of side length ${\varepsilon }^{\prime }{2}^{j}\Phi /\left( {{10\alpha }{d}_{u}}\right)$ in ${V}_{i,j};$

		${V}_{i,j} = {Q}_{i,j} \smallsetminus  {Q}_{i,j - 1};\;{\bar{V}}_{i,j} \leftarrow$在${V}_{i,j};$中边长为${\varepsilon }^{\prime }{2}^{j}\Phi /\left( {{10\alpha }{d}_{u}}\right)$的网格

	对于每个$\square  \in  {\bar{V}}_{i}$执行

		if $\phi \left( {{x}_{i},\square }\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {X,\square }\right)  + \operatorname{diam}\left( \square \right)$ then

			$\operatorname{Arr}\left( G\right)  \leftarrow$ arrangement of $G;\;\overline{\operatorname{Arr}}\left( G\right)  \leftarrow$ complement of $\operatorname{Arr}\left( G\right)$ ;

			$\operatorname{Arr}\left( G\right)  \leftarrow$对$\operatorname{Arr}\left( G\right)$的补集$G;\;\overline{\operatorname{Arr}}\left( G\right)  \leftarrow$进行排列；

			${\operatorname{Arr}}^{\prime }\left( G\right)  \leftarrow$ partition of $\overline{\operatorname{Arr}}\left( G\right)$ into hyper-rectangles;

			对于每个$\mathcal{R} \in  {\operatorname{Arr}}^{\prime }\left( G\right)$执行

$\mathcal{S} = {\mathrm{{GkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }\left( \mathcal{C}\right)$ (or $\mathcal{S} = {\mathrm{{DkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }\left( \mathcal{C}\right)$ for the discrete version);

$\mathcal{S} = {\mathrm{{GkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }\left( \mathcal{C}\right)$（或离散版本中的$\mathcal{S} = {\mathrm{{DkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }\left( \mathcal{C}\right)$）；

<!-- Media -->

In this section we propose two algorithms that take as input $X$ and $r$ and return a set $\mathcal{S} \subset$ ${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$ of cardinality $\left| \mathcal{S}\right|  = k$ such that ${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ ,i.e., $\mathcal{S}$ is a $(1 +$ $\varepsilon )\gamma$ -approximation for the $k$ -median problem in ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ . They also return a number ${r}_{u}$ such that ${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ . We note that given a set of centers $S$ ,we cannot compute the error ${\mathbf{v}}_{S}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ efficiently,hence ${r}_{u}$ is needed to estimate the error of the clustering. We also note that if ${\mathbf{A}}_{u} = \mathbf{A}$ ,then the returned set $\mathcal{S}$ is an $\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$ approximation solution for the relational $k$ -median clustering problem on $\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$ . For the discrete relational $k$ -median clustering, we return $\mathcal{S} \subseteq  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ and ${r}_{u}$ such that, ${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ .

本节提出两种算法，其输入为$X$和$r$，输出一个基数为$\left| \mathcal{S}\right|  = k$的集合$\mathcal{S} \subset$${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$，使得${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$，即$\mathcal{S}$是${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$中$k$-中值问题的$(1 +$$\varepsilon )\gamma$近似解。算法同时返回数值${r}_{u}$以满足${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$。需注意，给定中心点集$S$时，我们无法高效计算误差${\mathbf{v}}_{S}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$，因此需要${r}_{u}$来估计聚类误差。若${\mathbf{A}}_{u} = \mathbf{A}$，则返回集合$\mathcal{S}$构成$\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$上关系型$k$-中值聚类问题的$\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$近似解。对于离散关系型$k$-中值聚类，我们返回$\mathcal{S} \subseteq  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$和${r}_{u}$，满足${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$。

The first algorithm is a slow deterministic algorithm that runs in $\Omega \left( {{\left| X\right| }^{{d}_{u} + 1}N}\right)$ time. The second algorithm is a faster randomized algorithm that runs in time roughly $O\left( {\left| X\right| N}\right)$ time. We mostly focus on the geometric version of the relational $k$ -median clustering,however,we always highlight the differences with the discrete version.

第一个算法是耗时$\Omega \left( {{\left| X\right| }^{{d}_{u} + 1}N}\right)$的慢速确定性算法，第二个是耗时约$O\left( {\left| X\right| N}\right)$的快速随机算法。我们主要研究关系型$k$-中值聚类的几何版本，但会始终强调其与离散版本的区别。

### 3.1 Slow deterministic algorithm

### 3.1 慢速确定性算法

For the slow deterministic algorithm we construct a hierarchical grid around every center ${x}_{i} \in  X$ . Then,for every cell that is close enough to ${x}_{i}$ ,we compute the number of join results in ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ inside the cell that do not lie inside another cell previously processed by our algorithm. We count the number of join results using Lemma 2.1.

对于慢速确定性算法，我们在每个中心点${x}_{i} \in  X$周围构建分层网格。对于每个足够接近${x}_{i}$的网格单元，计算其中属于${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$的连接结果数量（这些结果不位于算法先前处理过的其他单元内）。该计数过程采用引理2.1实现。

Algorithm. The pseudocode of this algorithm is shown in Algorithm 1. We first set ${\varepsilon }^{\prime } = \varepsilon /4$ and define $\Phi  = \frac{r}{\alpha n}$ as a lower bound estimate of the average radius $\frac{{\mathrm{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }{n}$ . For every point ${x}_{i} \in  X$ we construct an exponential grid around ${x}_{i}$ . Let ${Q}_{i,j}$ be an axis parallel square with side length $\Phi  \cdot  {2}^{j}$ centered at ${x}_{i}$ ,for $j = 0,1,\ldots ,2\log \left( {\alpha n}\right)$ . Let ${V}_{i,0} = {Q}_{i,0}$ and let ${V}_{i,j} = {Q}_{i,j} \smallsetminus  {Q}_{i,j - 1}$ . We partition ${V}_{i,j}$ into a grid ${\bar{V}}_{i,j}$ of side length ${\varepsilon }^{\prime }\Phi {2}^{j}/\left( {{10\alpha }{d}_{u}}\right)$ . Let ${\bar{V}}_{i} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{j}{\bar{V}}_{i,j}$ .

算法。该算法的伪代码如算法1所示。我们首先设定${\varepsilon }^{\prime } = \varepsilon /4$，并将$\Phi  = \frac{r}{\alpha n}$定义为平均半径$\frac{{\mathrm{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }{n}$的下界估计值。对于每个点${x}_{i} \in  X$，我们在${x}_{i}$周围构建指数网格。设${Q}_{i,j}$为以${x}_{i}$为中心、边长为$\Phi  \cdot  {2}^{j}$的轴平行正方形（对于$j = 0,1,\ldots ,2\log \left( {\alpha n}\right)$）。令${V}_{i,0} = {Q}_{i,0}$且${V}_{i,j} = {Q}_{i,j} \smallsetminus  {Q}_{i,j - 1}$。我们将${V}_{i,j}$划分为边长为${\varepsilon }^{\prime }\Phi {2}^{j}/\left( {{10\alpha }{d}_{u}}\right)$的网格${\bar{V}}_{i,j}$。设${\bar{V}}_{i} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{j}{\bar{V}}_{i,j}$。

It holds that $\phi \left( {{x}_{i},{\square }_{1}}\right)  >$ $\phi \left( {{x}_{i + 1},{\square }_{1}}\right)  + \operatorname{diam}\left( {\square }_{1}\right)$ so ${\square }_{1}$ is not processed by the algorithm. ${x}_{i + 2}$ is the closest center to ${\square }_{2}$ ,i.e., $\phi \left( {X,{\square }_{2}}\right)  = \phi \left( {{x}_{i + 2},{\square }_{2}}\right)$ and it holds $\phi \left( {{x}_{i},{\square }_{2}}\right)  <$ $\phi \left( {{x}_{i + 2},{\square }_{2}}\right)  + \operatorname{diam}\left( {\square }_{2}\right)$ ,so ${\square }_{2}$ is processed by the algorithm. The red (blue) dashed segments represent the distances of ${x}_{i}$ to ${\square }_{1}\left( {\square }_{2}\right) ,{x}_{i + 1}\left( {x}_{i + 2}\right)$ to ${\square }_{1}\left( {\square }_{2}\right)$ ,and the diameter of ${\square }_{1}\left( {\square }_{2}\right)$ .

图2。设${\square }_{1},{\square }_{2} \in  {\bar{V}}_{i}$。由于$\phi \left( {{x}_{i},{\square }_{1}}\right)  >$$\phi \left( {{x}_{i + 1},{\square }_{1}}\right)  + \operatorname{diam}\left( {\square }_{1}\right)$成立，故${\square }_{1}$未被算法处理。${x}_{i + 2}$是距${\square }_{2}$最近的聚类中心，即$\phi \left( {X,{\square }_{2}}\right)  = \phi \left( {{x}_{i + 2},{\square }_{2}}\right)$，且满足$\phi \left( {{x}_{i},{\square }_{2}}\right)  <$$\phi \left( {{x}_{i + 2},{\square }_{2}}\right)  + \operatorname{diam}\left( {\square }_{2}\right)$，因此${\square }_{2}$被算法处理。红色（蓝色）虚线段分别表示${x}_{i}$到${\square }_{1}\left( {\square }_{2}\right) ,{x}_{i + 1}\left( {x}_{i + 2}\right)$的距离及${\square }_{1}\left( {\square }_{2}\right)$的直径。

An example of the grid construction can be seen in Figure 1. The construction so far is similar to the exponential grid in [32]. However, from now on the algorithm and the analysis is different. Let $G = \varnothing$ be an empty set of grid cells. Let also $\mathcal{C} = \varnothing$ be an empty point set in ${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$ and $w$ be a weight function that we are going to define on $C$ .

网格构建示例如图1所示。目前的构建过程与文献[32]中的指数网格类似。但从现在开始，算法及分析将有所不同。设$G = \varnothing$为空网格单元集合，$\mathcal{C} = \varnothing$为${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$中的空点集，$w$为我们将要在$C$上定义的权重函数。

For every ${x}_{i} \in  X$ ,and for every cell $\square  \in  {\bar{V}}_{i}$ we repeat the following steps. Let $\operatorname{diam}\left( \square \right)$ be the diameter of $\square$ . If

对于每个${x}_{i} \in  X$及每个网格单元$\square  \in  {\bar{V}}_{i}$，我们重复以下步骤：设$\operatorname{diam}\left( \square \right)$为$\square$的直径。若

$$
\phi \left( {{x}_{i},\square }\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {X,\square }\right)  + \operatorname{diam}\left( \square \right) , \tag{3}
$$

(an example of applying Equation (3) is shown in Figure 2) then we proceed as follows. Let ${G}_{\square }$ be the set $G$ just before the algorithm processes the cell $\square$ . The goal is to identify $\left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {\square  \smallsetminus  {G}_{\square }}\right) }\right|$ and if $\left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {\square  \smallsetminus  {G}_{\square }}\right) }\right|  > 0$ then add a representative point ${s}_{\square } \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {\square  \smallsetminus  {G}_{\square }}\right)$ in $\mathcal{C}$ with weight $w\left( {s}_{\square }\right)  = \left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {\square  \smallsetminus  {G}_{\square }}\right) }\right|$ . Next,we construct the arrangement of ${G}_{\square }$ . The arrangement [7,30] of ${G}_{\square }$ ,denoted $\operatorname{Arr}\left( {G}_{\square }\right)$ ,is a partitioning of ${G}_{\square }$ into rectangular contiguous regions,such that for every region reg in the arrangement,reg lies in the same subset of ${G}_{\square }$ . Let $\overline{\operatorname{Arr}}\left( {G}_{\square }\right)$ be the complement of $\operatorname{Arr}\left( {G}_{\square }\right)$ ,and let ${\operatorname{Arr}}^{\prime }\left( {G}_{\square }\right)$ be a partition of $\overline{\operatorname{Arr}}\left( {G}_{\square }\right)$ into hyper-rectangles. For each rectangle $\mathcal{R} \in  {\operatorname{Arr}}^{\prime }\left( {G}_{\square }\right)$ ,we compute ${\square }_{\mathcal{R}} = \square  \cap  \mathcal{R}$ ,which is also a hyper-rectangle in ${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$ . Using the CountRect $\left( \cdot \right)$ procedure form Lemma 2.1 we compute ${K}_{\square } = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\mathcal{R} \in  {\operatorname{Arr}}^{\prime }\left( G\right) }}\left| {{\square }_{\mathcal{R}} \cap  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right|$ . If ${K}_{\square } > 0$ we also get an arbitrary point ${s}_{\square }$ from $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{\mathcal{R} \in  {\operatorname{Arr}}^{\prime }\left( G\right) }}{\square }_{\mathcal{R}} \cap  {q}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$ . We add ${s}_{\square }$ in $C$ and we set its weight $w\left( {s}_{\square }\right)  = {K}_{\square }$ . We say that all tuples in ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {\square  \smallsetminus  {G}_{\square }}\right)$ are assigned to ${s}_{\square }$ ,and we add $\square$ in $G$ . An example of the arrangement can be seen in Figure 3.

（应用公式(3)的示例见图2）接下来按如下步骤进行。设${G}_{\square }$为算法处理单元格$\square$前的集合$G$。目标是识别$\left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {\square  \smallsetminus  {G}_{\square }}\right) }\right|$，若满足条件$\left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {\square  \smallsetminus  {G}_{\square }}\right) }\right|  > 0$，则在$\mathcal{C}$中添加具有权重$w\left( {s}_{\square }\right)  = \left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {\square  \smallsetminus  {G}_{\square }}\right) }\right|$的代表点${s}_{\square } \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {\square  \smallsetminus  {G}_{\square }}\right)$。随后构建${G}_{\square }$的空间排列。该排列[7,30]记作$\operatorname{Arr}\left( {G}_{\square }\right)$，是将${G}_{\square }$划分为连续矩形区域的分割方式，使得每个区域reg在排列中均位于${G}_{\square }$的相同子集内。令$\overline{\operatorname{Arr}}\left( {G}_{\square }\right)$为$\operatorname{Arr}\left( {G}_{\square }\right)$的补集，${\operatorname{Arr}}^{\prime }\left( {G}_{\square }\right)$是将$\overline{\operatorname{Arr}}\left( {G}_{\square }\right)$划分为超矩形的分区。对每个矩形$\mathcal{R} \in  {\operatorname{Arr}}^{\prime }\left( {G}_{\square }\right)$计算${\square }_{\mathcal{R}} = \square  \cap  \mathcal{R}$（该值也是${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$中的超矩形）。借助引理2.1的CountRect过程$\left( \cdot \right)$计算${K}_{\square } = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\mathcal{R} \in  {\operatorname{Arr}}^{\prime }\left( G\right) }}\left| {{\square }_{\mathcal{R}} \cap  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right|$。若满足条件${K}_{\square } > 0$，则从$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{\mathcal{R} \in  {\operatorname{Arr}}^{\prime }\left( G\right) }}{\square }_{\mathcal{R}} \cap  {q}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$获取任意点${s}_{\square }$。将该点加入$C$并设置权重$w\left( {s}_{\square }\right)  = {K}_{\square }$。称${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {\square  \smallsetminus  {G}_{\square }}\right)$中所有元组均分配给${s}_{\square }$，并将$\square$加入$G$。该排列示例见图3。

On the other hand,if the condition (3) is not satisfied,then we skip $\square$ and we continue with the next cell in the exponential grid.

反之，若不满足条件(3)，则跳过$\square$并继续处理指数网格中的下一单元格。

In the end,after repeating the algorithm for each ${x}_{i} \in  X$ ,we get a weighted set $\mathcal{C}$ . We run the standard algorithm for the weighted $k$ -median problem ${\mathrm{{GkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$ (or ${\mathrm{{DkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$ for the discrete $k$ -median problem) on $\mathcal{C}$ to get a set of $k$ centers $\mathcal{S}$ ,and we return $\mathcal{S}$ as the answer. Furthermore,we return ${r}_{u} = \frac{1}{1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( C\right)$ .

最终，在对每个${x}_{i} \in  X$重复执行算法后，我们得到一个加权集合$\mathcal{C}$。我们在$\mathcal{C}$上运行加权$k$-中位数问题（对于离散$k$-中位数问题则使用${\mathrm{{DkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$）的标准算法${\mathrm{{GkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$，得到一组$k$中心点$\mathcal{S}$，并将$\mathcal{S}$作为答案返回。此外，我们还返回${r}_{u} = \frac{1}{1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( C\right)$。
