<!-- Media -->

Correctness. We first show that every tuple in ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ is assigned to a point in $\mathcal{C}$ . Then,we show that $\mathcal{C}$ is an ${\varepsilon }^{\prime }$ -coreset for ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right)$ . Next,we show that $\mathcal{S}$ is a good approximation of the optimum $k$ -median solution and ${r}_{u}$ is a good approximation of ${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)$ . Finally,we show that ${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ . All missing proofs can be found in Appendix B.

正确性证明。首先证明${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$中的每个元组都被分配到$\mathcal{C}$中的某个点。接着证明$\mathcal{C}$是${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right)$的${\varepsilon }^{\prime }$-核心集。然后证明$\mathcal{S}$是最优$k$-中位数解的良好近似，且${r}_{u}$是${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)$的良好近似。最后证明${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$。所有缺失的证明见附录B。

LEMMA 3.1. Every tuple $t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ is assigned to a point in $\mathcal{C}$ . Furthermore,the number of tuples in ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ that are assigned to a point $s \in  \mathcal{C}$ is $w\left( s\right)$ .

引理3.1. 每个元组$t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$都被分配到$\mathcal{C}$中的某个点。此外，${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$中被分配到点$s \in  \mathcal{C}$的元组数量为$w\left( s\right)$。

Proof. Let ${x}_{i} \in  X$ be the center that is closest to $t$ . By definition,there will be a cell $\square$ defined by the exponential grid around ${x}_{i}$ that contains $t$ ,since $\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i}}\right)  \leq  {\alpha n\Phi }$ . We show that for the cell $\square$ the condition (3) holds. Let ${x}_{j}$ be the center such that $\phi \left( {{x}_{j},\square }\right)  = \phi \left( {X,\square }\right)$ . We have $\phi \left( {{x}_{i},\square }\right)  \leq$ $\phi \left( {{x}_{i},t}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {{x}_{j},t}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {X,\square }\right)  + \operatorname{diam}\left( \square \right)$ . Hence, $t$ is assigned in ${s}_{\square }$ . The second part of the lemma holds by definition.

证明。设${x}_{i} \in  X$为距离$t$最近的聚类中心。根据定义，由于$\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i}}\right)  \leq  {\alpha n\Phi }$，在${x}_{i}$周围的指数网格中将存在包含$t$的单元$\square$。我们证明该单元$\square$满足条件(3)。令${x}_{j}$为满足$\phi \left( {{x}_{j},\square }\right)  = \phi \left( {X,\square }\right)$的中心点，可得$\phi \left( {{x}_{i},\square }\right)  \leq$$\phi \left( {{x}_{i},t}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {{x}_{j},t}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {X,\square }\right)  + \operatorname{diam}\left( \square \right)$。因此，$t$被分配到${s}_{\square }$中。引理的第二部分根据定义成立。

For a tuple $t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ ,let ${\square }_{t}$ be the cell from the exponential grid defined by the algorithm such that ${\square }_{t}$ is the first cell processed by the algorithm that contains $t$ ,i.e., $t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {{\square }_{t} \smallsetminus  {G}_{{\square }_{t}}}\right)$ . Next, we denote the assignment of each tuple $t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ to a point in $\mathcal{C}$ by $\sigma$ ,i.e., $\sigma \left( t\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {s}_{{\square }_{t}} \in  \mathcal{C}$ . Let $i\left( t\right)$ be the index such that ${\square }_{t} \in  {\bar{V}}_{i\left( t\right) }$ . By definition,notice that ${x}_{i\left( t\right) } \in  X$ is the center visited by our algorithm at the moment that $t$ is assigned to $\sigma \left( t\right)$ .

对于元组$t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$，令${\square }_{t}$表示算法定义的指数网格中首个被处理且包含$t$的单元，即$t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {{\square }_{t} \smallsetminus  {G}_{{\square }_{t}}}\right)$。接着，我们用$\sigma$表示每个元组$t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$到$\mathcal{C}$中某点的分配关系，即$\sigma \left( t\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {s}_{{\square }_{t}} \in  \mathcal{C}$。设$i\left( t\right)$为满足${\square }_{t} \in  {\bar{V}}_{i\left( t\right) }$的索引。注意根据定义，当$t$被分配到$\sigma \left( t\right)$时，${x}_{i\left( t\right) } \in  X$正是算法当前访问的聚类中心。

LEMMA 3.2. $C$ is an ${\varepsilon }^{\prime }$ -coreset for ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$ .

引理3.2。$C$是${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$的${\varepsilon }^{\prime }$-核心集。

Proof. Let $Y$ be an arbitrary set of $k$ points in ${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$ . The error is defined as $\mathcal{E} =  \mid  {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  -$ ${\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( \mathcal{C}\right) \left| { \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }}}\right| \phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  - \phi \left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right)  \mid$ . By the triangle inequality, $\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)$ and $\phi \left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)$ . Hence, $\left| {\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  - \phi \left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right|  \leq  \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)$ . We have,

证明。设$Y$为${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$中任意$k$点集。误差定义为$\mathcal{E} =  \mid  {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  -$${\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( \mathcal{C}\right) \left| { \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }}}\right| \phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  - \phi \left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right)  \mid$。根据三角不等式，$\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)$且$\phi \left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)$。因此，$\left| {\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  - \phi \left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right|  \leq  \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)$。可得：

$$
\mathcal{E} \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) ,\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  \leq  \Phi }}\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right) \; + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) ,\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  > \Phi }}\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right) .
$$

For $\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  \leq  \Phi$ ,by the construction of the exponential grid,we have $\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \operatorname{diam}\left( {\square }_{t}\right)  \leq$

对于$\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  \leq  \Phi$，根据指数网格的构造原理，我们有$\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \operatorname{diam}\left( {\square }_{t}\right)  \leq$

$\frac{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{10\alpha }\Phi$ ,hence

$\frac{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{10\alpha }\Phi$，故

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) ,\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  \leq  \Phi }}\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{10\alpha }{n\Phi } \leq  \frac{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{10\alpha }{\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) .
$$

For $\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  > \Phi$ ,by the construction of the exponential grid we have $\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \operatorname{diam}\left( {\square }_{t}\right)  \leq$ $\frac{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{10\alpha }\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)$ . Notice that $\phi \left( {{x}_{i\left( t\right) },{\square }_{t}}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {X,{\square }_{t}}\right)  + \operatorname{diam}\left( {\square }_{t}\right)$ ,by condition (3). We have,

对于$\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  > \Phi$，依据指数网格构造可得$\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \operatorname{diam}\left( {\square }_{t}\right)  \leq$$\frac{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{10\alpha }\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)$。注意到根据条件(3)，$\phi \left( {{x}_{i\left( t\right) },{\square }_{t}}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {X,{\square }_{t}}\right)  + \operatorname{diam}\left( {\square }_{t}\right)$。可得：

$$
\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {{x}_{i\left( t\right) },{\square }_{t}}\right)  + \operatorname{diam}\left( {\square }_{t}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {X,{\square }_{t}}\right)  + 2\operatorname{diam}\left( {\square }_{t}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {t,X}\right)  + \frac{2{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{10\alpha }\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right) 
$$

$$
 \Leftrightarrow  \phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  \leq  \frac{1}{1 - \frac{2{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{10\alpha }}\phi \left( {t,X}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \frac{4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{10\alpha }}\right) \phi \left( {t,X}\right) ,
$$

so $\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{10\alpha }\left( {1 + \frac{4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{10\alpha }}\right) \phi \left( {t,X}\right)$ . Then,we get

因此$\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{10\alpha }\left( {1 + \frac{4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{10\alpha }}\right) \phi \left( {t,X}\right)$。继而得到

$$
\mathcal{E} \leq  \frac{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{10\alpha }{\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  + \frac{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{10\alpha }\left( {1 + \frac{4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{10\alpha }}\right) {\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {\frac{2{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{10} + \frac{4{\left( {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\right) }^{2}}{100\alpha }}\right) {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) 
$$

$$
 \leq  {\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) .
$$

This implies that $\left| {{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)  - {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( \mathcal{C}\right) }\right|  \leq  {\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)$ .

这意味着$\left| {{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)  - {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( \mathcal{C}\right) }\right|  \leq  {\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)$。

From the previous lemma, we conclude with the main result establishing the correctness of the algorithm.

由前述引理，我们最终得出确立算法正确性的主要结论。

LEMMA 3.3. If ${\mathrm{{GkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$ is used,then $\mathcal{S} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ and ${v}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {v}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ . If ${\mathrm{{DkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$ is used,then $\mathcal{S} \subseteq  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ and ${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{{\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{disc}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) \left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ .

引理3.3. 若使用${\mathrm{{GkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$，则$\mathcal{S} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$且${v}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {v}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$。若使用${\mathrm{{DkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$，则$\mathcal{S} \subseteq  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$且${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{{\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{disc}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) \left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$。

Proof. By the definition of ${\mathrm{{GkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$ and Lemma 3.2,we have ${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( \mathcal{C}\right)  \leq  \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( \mathcal{C}\right) }\left( \mathcal{C}\right)  \leq$ $\gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( \mathcal{C}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ ,and ${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( \mathcal{C}\right)  \geq  \left( {1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ ,so

证明. 根据${\mathrm{{GkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$的定义及引理3.2，可得${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( \mathcal{C}\right)  \leq  \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( \mathcal{C}\right) }\left( \mathcal{C}\right)  \leq$$\gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( \mathcal{C}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$，且${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( \mathcal{C}\right)  \geq  \left( {1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$，故

$$
{\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{1}{1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( \mathcal{C}\right)  = {r}_{u} \leq  \gamma \frac{1}{1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( \mathcal{C}\right) }\left( \mathcal{C}\right)  \leq  \frac{1 + {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) 
$$

$$
 \leq  \left( {1 + 4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  = \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) .
$$

The proof using the DkMedian ${\mathrm{{Alg}}}_{\gamma }$ algorithm is shown in Appendix B.

使用DkMedian${\mathrm{{Alg}}}_{\gamma }$算法的证明过程见附录B。

Running time.

运行时间.

LEMMA 3.4. $\left| \mathcal{C}\right|  = O\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log N}\right)$ .

引理3.4. $\left| \mathcal{C}\right|  = O\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log N}\right)$.

Proof. For each ${x}_{i} \in  X$ there are $2\log \left( {\alpha N}\right)  = O\left( {\log N}\right)$ boxes ${Q}_{i,j}$ . For each ${V}_{i,j}$ ,we construct $O\left( {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\right)$ cells. In the worst case, $\mathcal{C}$ contains a point for every cell.

证明. 对于每个${x}_{i} \in  X$存在$2\log \left( {\alpha N}\right)  = O\left( {\log N}\right)$个盒状结构${Q}_{i,j}$。针对每个${V}_{i,j}$，我们构建$O\left( {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\right)$个单元。最坏情况下，$\mathcal{C}$会为每个单元包含一个点。

In total,the algorithm visits $O\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log N}\right)$ cells. For each cell $\square$ ,we check the condition (3) in $O\left( \left| X\right| \right)$ time and we compute the arrangement $\operatorname{Arr}\left( {G}_{\square }\right)$ in $O\left( {{\left| X\right| }^{{d}_{u}}{\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}^{2}}{\log }^{{d}_{u}}N}\right)$ time. For each hyper-rectangle in the complement,we run a query as in Lemma 2.1 in $O\left( {N\log N}\right)$ time. Overall, $C$ is constructed in $O\left( {{\left| X\right| }^{{d}_{u} + 1}{\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}^{2} - {d}_{u}}N{\log }^{{d}_{u} + 2}N}\right)$ time. Finally,we get $\mathcal{S}$ in $O\left( {{T}_{Y}^{\mathrm{{med}}}\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log N}\right) }\right)$ time.

该算法总共访问$O\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log N}\right)$个单元。对于每个单元$\square$，我们以$O\left( \left| X\right| \right)$时间检查条件(3)，并以$O\left( {{\left| X\right| }^{{d}_{u}}{\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}^{2}}{\log }^{{d}_{u}}N}\right)$时间计算排列$\operatorname{Arr}\left( {G}_{\square }\right)$。对于补集中的每个超矩形，我们以$O\left( {N\log N}\right)$时间运行如引理2.1所述的查询。整体而言，$C$的构建耗时$O\left( {{\left| X\right| }^{{d}_{u} + 1}{\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}^{2} - {d}_{u}}N{\log }^{{d}_{u} + 2}N}\right)$。最终，我们在$O\left( {{T}_{Y}^{\mathrm{{med}}}\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log N}\right) }\right)$时间内获得$\mathcal{S}$。

THEOREM 3.5. Let $\mathbf{D}$ be a database instance with $N$ tuples, $\mathbf{q}$ be an acyclic join query over a set of attributes $\mathbf{A}$ and ${\mathbf{A}}_{u} \subseteq  \mathbf{A}$ . Given a set $X \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,a constant $\alpha$ such that ${\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \alpha {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ , and a constant parameter $\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,there exists an algorithm that computes a set $\mathcal{S} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ of $k$ points and a number ${r}_{u}$ in $O\left( {{\left| X\right| }^{{d}_{u} + 1}N{\log }^{{d}_{u} + 2}N + {T}_{Y}^{\text{med }}\left( {\left| X\right| \log N}\right) }\right)$ time such that ${v}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  (1 +$ ε) $\gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ . There also exists an algorithm that computes a set $\mathcal{S} \subseteq  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ of $k$ points and a number ${r}_{u}$ with the same running time,such that ${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{{\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) \left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ .

定理3.5。设$\mathbf{D}$为包含$N$个元组的数据库实例，$\mathbf{q}$为属性集$\mathbf{A}$和${\mathbf{A}}_{u} \subseteq  \mathbf{A}$上的无环连接查询。给定集合$X \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$、常数$\alpha$满足${\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \alpha {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$、以及常数参数$\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$，存在一个算法可在$O\left( {{\left| X\right| }^{{d}_{u} + 1}N{\log }^{{d}_{u} + 2}N + {T}_{Y}^{\text{med }}\left( {\left| X\right| \log N}\right) }\right)$时间内计算出包含$k$个点的集合$\mathcal{S} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$和数值${r}_{u}$，使得${v}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  (1 +$ε)$\gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$。另存在一个算法可在相同时间内计算出包含$k$个点的集合$\mathcal{S} \subseteq  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$和数值${r}_{u}$，满足${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{{\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) \left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$。

### 3.2 Fast randomized algorithm

### 3.2 快速随机算法

As we show in Section 4.1, the algorithm in the previous section can be used to get a constant approximation for the relational $k$ -median problem. However,the running time is $\Omega \left( {{\left| X\right| }^{{d}_{u} + 1}N}\right)$ . As we will see in the next section $\left| X\right|  = {k}^{2}$ ,leading to an $\Omega \left( {{k}^{2{d}_{u} + 2}N}\right)$ algorithm. In this section, we propose a more involved randomized algorithm that improves the factor ${\left| X\right| }^{{d}_{u} + 1}N$ to only $\left| X\right|  \cdot  N$ . Undoubtedly,the expensive part of the deterministic algorithm is the cardinality estimation $\left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {\square  \smallsetminus  {G}_{\square }}\right) }\right|$ . Next,we design a faster algorithm to overcome this obstacle. The algorithm constructs exactly the same exponential grid as described above. However, in this algorithm, we use a more involved approach to estimate the weights $w\left( {s}_{\square }\right)$ faster using random uniform sampling. We use the same notation as in the previous subsection. Let $\mathcal{C} = \varnothing$ and let $\Phi$ as defined above. In this algorithm, we will characterize each cell we visit as heavy or light. Let $B$ denote the set of the processed heavy cells. So,we initialize with $B = \varnothing$ .

如第4.1节所示，前节算法可用于获得关系型$k$-中位数问题的常数近似解，但其时间复杂度高达$\Omega \left( {{\left| X\right| }^{{d}_{u} + 1}N}\right)$。下文将展示如何通过$\left| X\right|  = {k}^{2}$实现$\Omega \left( {{k}^{2{d}_{u} + 2}N}\right)$算法。本节提出更复杂的随机化算法，将系数${\left| X\right| }^{{d}_{u} + 1}N$降至$\left| X\right|  \cdot  N$。显然，确定性算法的性能瓶颈在于基数估计$\left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {\square  \smallsetminus  {G}_{\square }}\right) }\right|$。为此我们设计快速算法突破该限制：虽然仍构建相同指数网格，但采用均匀随机采样加速权重$w\left( {s}_{\square }\right)$估算。沿用前文符号定义，设$\mathcal{C} = \varnothing$且$\Phi$如前所述。本算法将遍历单元标记为重型或轻型，用$B$表示已处理重型单元集合，初始化时$B = \varnothing$。

Algorithm. The pseudocode of the algorithm is shown in Algorithm 2. We set ${\varepsilon }^{\prime } = \varepsilon /{34}$ . For each ${x}_{i} \in  X$ and for each cell $\square  \in  {\bar{V}}_{i}$ ,we check condition (3). If it is satisfied,then we process $\square$ . Otherwise,we skip it and continue with the next cell. Let $\square$ be a cell in the exponential grid that the algorithm processes. We compute ${n}_{\square } = \left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \cap  \square }\right|$ and we set $\tau  = \frac{1}{{16}\left| X\right| {\left( {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\right) }^{-{d}_{u} - 1}\log N}$ . Using the algorithm from Lemma 2.1,we sample with replacement a multi-set ${H}_{\square }$ of $M = \frac{3}{{\left( {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\right) }^{2}\tau }\log \left( {2{N}^{10d}}\right)  =$ $O\left( {\left| X\right| {\left( {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\right) }^{-{d}_{u} - 3}{\log }^{2}N}\right)$ points from ${q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \square$ and we set ${g}_{\square } = \left| {{H}_{\square } \smallsetminus  \left( {B \cap  {H}_{\square }}\right) }\right|$ ,i.e.,the number of samples that are not currently contained in heavy cells we processed. If $\frac{{g}_{\square }}{M} \geq  {2\tau }$ ,then let ${s}_{\square }$ be any of the sampled points in ${H}_{\square } \smallsetminus  B$ as the representative point of $\square$ . We add ${s}_{\square }$ in $C$ with weight $w\left( {s}_{\square }\right)  = \frac{1}{1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }} \cdot  \frac{{g}_{\square }}{M} \cdot  {n}_{\square }$ . We say that all the points in ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {\square  \smallsetminus  B}\right)$ are mapped to ${s}_{\square }$ . We characterize $\square$ as a heavy cell and we add it to $B$ . Otherwise,if $\frac{{g}_{\square }}{M} < {2\tau }$ ,then $\square$ is a light cell, we skip it and continue processing the next cell. An example of the sampling procedure is shown in Figure 4. In the end,after repeating the algorithm for each ${x}_{i} \in  X$ ,we have a weighted set $\mathcal{C}$ of size $O\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log n}\right)$ . We run the standard algorithm for the weighted $k$ -median problem ${\mathrm{{GkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$ (or discrete $k$ -median problem ${\mathrm{{DkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$ ) on $C$ to get a set of $k$ centers $\mathcal{S}$ . We return the set of centers $\mathcal{S}$ . Furthermore,we return ${r}_{u} = \frac{1 + 4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{1 - 9{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( \mathcal{C}\right)$ .

算法。该算法的伪代码如算法2所示。我们设定${\varepsilon }^{\prime } = \varepsilon /{34}$。对于每个${x}_{i} \in  X$及每个单元$\square  \in  {\bar{V}}_{i}$，我们检查条件(3)。若满足，则处理$\square$；否则跳过并继续下一单元。设$\square$为算法处理的指数网格中的单元，我们计算${n}_{\square } = \left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \cap  \square }\right|$并设定$\tau  = \frac{1}{{16}\left| X\right| {\left( {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\right) }^{-{d}_{u} - 1}\log N}$。利用引理2.1的算法，我们从${q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \square$中有放回地抽样生成包含$M = \frac{3}{{\left( {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\right) }^{2}\tau }\log \left( {2{N}^{10d}}\right)  =$$O\left( {\left| X\right| {\left( {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\right) }^{-{d}_{u} - 3}{\log }^{2}N}\right)$个点的多重集${H}_{\square }$，并设定${g}_{\square } = \left| {{H}_{\square } \smallsetminus  \left( {B \cap  {H}_{\square }}\right) }\right|$（即当前未包含在已处理密集单元中的样本数）。若$\frac{{g}_{\square }}{M} \geq  {2\tau }$，则任选${H}_{\square } \smallsetminus  B$中一个采样点${s}_{\square }$作为$\square$的代表点，以权重$w\left( {s}_{\square }\right)  = \frac{1}{1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }} \cdot  \frac{{g}_{\square }}{M} \cdot  {n}_{\square }$将其加入$C$。此时称${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {\square  \smallsetminus  B}\right)$内所有点都映射到${s}_{\square }$，并将$\square$标记为密集单元加入$B$。反之若$\frac{{g}_{\square }}{M} < {2\tau }$，则$\square$为稀疏单元，跳过并处理下一单元。图4展示了抽样过程示例。最终对每个${x}_{i} \in  X$重复算法后，得到大小为$O\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log n}\right)$的加权集$\mathcal{C}$。我们在$C$上运行标准加权$k$-中值问题${\mathrm{{GkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$（或离散$k$-中值问题${\mathrm{{DkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$）算法，获得$k$个中心点$\mathcal{S}$作为输出，同时返回${r}_{u} = \frac{1 + 4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{1 - 9{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( \mathcal{C}\right)$。

Correctness. Let ${P}_{u}$ (heavy tuples) be the tuples in ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ that belong to at least one heavy cell in $B$ and let ${\bar{P}}_{u} = {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \smallsetminus  {P}_{u}$ (light tuples),at the end of the algorithm. By construction,every tuple $t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ ,belongs to at least one heavy or light cell. Notice that a point $t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ might first lie in a light cell and only later in the algorithm it might be found in a heavy cell. It is straightforward to see that any heavy point $t \in  {P}_{u}$ is mapped to a point in $\mathcal{C}$ . For each $t \in  {P}_{u}$ ,let ${\square }_{t}$ be the first heavy cell visited by the algorithm such that $t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  {\square }_{t}$ . Let $i\left( t\right)$ be the index such that ${\square }_{t} \in  {\bar{V}}_{i\left( t\right) }$ . In the deterministic algorithm,we had the assignment function $\sigma \left( \cdot \right)$ for all the points in ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( D\right)$ . In this algorithm,we only map the points in ${P}_{u}$ ,so we define a new mapping function $\widehat{\sigma }\left( \cdot \right)$ ,i.e.,for a point $t \in  {P}_{u},\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {s}_{{\square }_{t}} \in  \mathcal{C}$ . For a cell $\square$ processed by the algorithm,let ${B}_{\square }$ be the set of heavy cells found by the algorithm just before $\square$ was processed. For a point $p \in  \mathcal{C}$ ,let ${\square }_{p}$ be the cell that the algorithm processed while $p$ was added in $\mathcal{C}$ ,and let ${n}_{p} = \left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {{\square }_{p} \smallsetminus  {B}_{{\square }_{p}}}\right) }\right|$ be the number of the points mapped to $p$ .

正确性。设${P}_{u}$（重元组）为${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$中属于$B$内至少一个重单元格的元组，设${\bar{P}}_{u} = {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \smallsetminus  {P}_{u}$（轻元组）为算法结束时不属于任何重单元格的元组。根据构造，每个元组$t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$都至少属于一个重单元格或轻单元格。注意，点$t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$可能最初位于轻单元格，仅在算法后期才被发现属于重单元格。显然，任何重点$t \in  {P}_{u}$都会被映射到$\mathcal{C}$中的点。对于每个$t \in  {P}_{u}$，设${\square }_{t}$是算法访问的首个满足$t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  {\square }_{t}$的重单元格。设$i\left( t\right)$为满足${\square }_{t} \in  {\bar{V}}_{i\left( t\right) }$的索引。在确定性算法中，我们对${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( D\right)$中所有点使用分配函数$\sigma \left( \cdot \right)$。本算法仅映射${P}_{u}$中的点，因此定义新映射函数$\widehat{\sigma }\left( \cdot \right)$，即对于点$t \in  {P}_{u},\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {s}_{{\square }_{t}} \in  \mathcal{C}$。对于算法处理的单元格$\square$，设${B}_{\square }$为处理$\square$前算法发现的重单元格集合。对于点$p \in  \mathcal{C}$，设${\square }_{p}$为算法处理时$p$被加入$\mathcal{C}$的单元格，设${n}_{p} = \left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {{\square }_{p} \smallsetminus  {B}_{{\square }_{p}}}\right) }\right|$为映射到$p$的点数。

We show the correctness of our algorithm through a number of technical lemmas. We first show a crucial observation. There exists a charging process where i) every light tuple (i.e., a tuple that does not belong to a heavy cell) charges $\frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}$ heavy tuples that lie to the same cell as the light tuple, and ii) every heavy tuple is charged at most once. This observation is then used to show that for any set of $k$ centers,the $k$ -median error of ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$ is close enough to the $k$ -median error with respect to the heavy tuples so we can safely ignore the other tuples. Using this argument, we prove that $C$ is a $9{\varepsilon }^{\prime }$ -coreset for the heavy points. Using all previous observations,we conclude that $\mathcal{S}$ is a $\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$ -approximation for the relational $k$ -median clustering. All missing lemmas and proofs can be found in Appendix C.

我们通过若干技术引理证明算法的正确性。首先给出关键观察：存在一个计费过程，其中i)每个轻元组（即不属于重单元格的元组）向同单元格内的$\frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}$个重元组计费，ii)每个重元组最多被计费一次。该观察结果用于证明：对于任意$k$个中心点集合，${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$的$k$-中位数误差与重元组的$k$-中位数误差足够接近，因此可安全忽略其他元组。基于此，我们证明$C$构成重点的$9{\varepsilon }^{\prime }$-核心集。综合上述观察，最终得出$\mathcal{S}$是关系型$k$-中位数聚类的$\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$近似解。所有缺失引理及证明详见附录C。

LEMMA 3.6. For every point $p \in  C,{n}_{p} \leq  w\left( p\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + 4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) {n}_{p}$ with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ .

引理3.6. 对于任意点$p \in  C,{n}_{p} \leq  w\left( p\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + 4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) {n}_{p}$，其概率至少为$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$。

Proof. Let $p \in  \mathcal{C}$ be a point in the coreset. By definition $\frac{{g}_{\square p}}{M} \geq  {2\tau }$ ,so using the Chernoff bound, as shown in [20] (Lemma 2),with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ it holds that

证明. 设$p \in  \mathcal{C}$为核心集中的一个点。根据定义$\frac{{g}_{\square p}}{M} \geq  {2\tau }$，应用切尔诺夫界（如文献[20]引理2所示），当概率不低于$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$时有

$$
\left( {1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) \left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {{\square }_{p} \smallsetminus  {B}_{{\square }_{p}}}\right) }\right|  \leq  \frac{{g}_{{\square }_{p}}}{M}{n}_{{\square }_{p}} \leq  \left( {1 + {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) \left| {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \cap  \left( {{\square }_{p} \smallsetminus  {B}_{{\square }_{p}}}\right) }\right| .
$$

Hence, ${n}_{p} \leq  w\left( p\right)  \leq  \frac{1 + {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{n}_{p} \leq  \left( {1 + 4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) {n}_{p}$ with probability at least $1 - 1/{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }$ .

因此，${n}_{p} \leq  w\left( p\right)  \leq  \frac{1 + {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{n}_{p} \leq  \left( {1 + 4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) {n}_{p}$的概率至少为$1 - 1/{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }$。

Let $L$ be the set of light cells found by the algorithm. For a cell $\square  \in  L$ ,let ${\mathcal{P}}_{\square }^{L}$ be the set of the points in ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \square$ that do not belong in a heavy cell at the time that the algorithm processes ☐. Notice that a point $p \in  {\mathcal{P}}_{\square }^{L}$ might also belong to ${P}_{u}$ (points that lie in at least one heavy cell) because $p$ was found inside a heavy cell later in the algorithm. In addition,we note that it might be possible that ${\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L} \cap  {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{h}}^{L} \neq  \varnothing$ ,for $j \neq  h$ . Furthermore,let ${\mathcal{P}}_{\square }^{B}$ be the set of points in ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \square$ that belong in at least one heavy cell at the time that the algorithm processes $\square$ .

设$L$为算法找到的轻单元集合。对于单元$\square  \in  L$，令${\mathcal{P}}_{\square }^{L}$表示算法处理☐时不属于任何重单元的${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \square$内点集。注意点$p \in  {\mathcal{P}}_{\square }^{L}$可能同时属于${P}_{u}$（位于至少一个重单元的点集），因为$p$在算法后续过程中被发现位于某个重单元内。此外，我们注意到对于$j \neq  h$可能存在${\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L} \cap  {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{h}}^{L} \neq  \varnothing$的情况。进一步地，令${\mathcal{P}}_{\square }^{B}$表示算法处理$\square$时属于至少一个重单元的${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \cap  \square$内点集。

In the next technical lemma, we show the existence of a charging process that is later used to estimate the $k$ -median error of $\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ using only the heavy tuples ${P}_{u}$ .

在接下来的技术引理中，我们展示了一种充电过程的存在性，该过程后续被用于仅通过重元组${P}_{u}$来估计$\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$的$k$-中位数误差。

LEMMA 3.7. There exists a charging process that works with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ having the following properties. For every cell $\square  \in  L$ ,each point $p \in  {\mathcal{P}}_{\square }^{L}$ charges $\frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}$ points in ${\mathcal{P}}_{\square }^{B}$ ,such that, in the end,every point $t \in  {P}_{u}$ has been charged at most once.

引理3.7. 存在一个充电过程，其以至少$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$的概率满足以下性质：对于每个单元$\square  \in  L$，每个点$p \in  {\mathcal{P}}_{\square }^{L}$会对${\mathcal{P}}_{\square }^{B}$中的$\frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}$个点进行充电，最终每个点$t \in  {P}_{u}$至多被充电一次。

Proof. Let $L = \left\{  {{\square }_{1},\ldots ,{\square }_{\eta }}\right\}$ be sorted in ascending order of $\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{\square }^{L}\right|$ ,i.e., $\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|  \leq  \left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j + 1}}^{L}\right|$ . It is sufficient to show that for every $j = 1,\ldots ,\eta ,\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{B}\right|  - \frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h < j}}\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{h}}^{L}\right|  \geq  \frac{1}{\varepsilon }\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|$ . Indeed,if this inequality holds for every $j$ ,then there are always at least $\frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|$ uncharged points in ${\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{\mathcal{B}}$ ,and we can charge each point in ${\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}$ to $\frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}$ points in ${\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{B}$ .

证明. 设$L = \left\{  {{\square }_{1},\ldots ,{\square }_{\eta }}\right\}$按$\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{\square }^{L}\right|$升序排列，即$\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|  \leq  \left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j + 1}}^{L}\right|$。只需证明对于每个$j = 1,\ldots ,\eta ,\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{B}\right|  - \frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h < j}}\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{h}}^{L}\right|  \geq  \frac{1}{\varepsilon }\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|$，该不等式成立。事实上，若该不等式对所有$j$成立，则${\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{\mathcal{B}}$中始终存在至少$\frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|$个未充电点，从而可将${\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}$中的每个点充电到${\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{B}$中的$\frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}$个点上。

Recall that ${\square }_{j} \in  L$ is a light cell because the algorithm found that the ratio $\frac{\left| {H}_{{\square }_{j}} \cap  {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right| }{M} \leq  {2\tau }$ . In this case,from the Chernoff bound (Lemma 2 in the full version of [20]) we have $\frac{\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathcal{D}}_{1}}^{L}\right| }{\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathcal{D}}_{1}}^{B}\right|  + \left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathcal{D}}_{1}}^{L}\right| } \leq  {4\tau }$ with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ . Solving the inequality with respect to $\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{B}\right|$ ,we get $\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{B}\right|  \geq$ $\frac{1 - {4\tau }}{4\tau }\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|  \geq  \frac{1}{8\tau }\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|$ ,because $\tau  < \frac{1}{8}$ . Hence, $\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{B}\right|  \geq  2\left| X\right| {\left( {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\right) }^{-{d}_{u} - 1}\log \left( N\right)  \cdot  \left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|$ .

回顾${\square }_{j} \in  L$是一个轻量级单元，因为算法发现比率$\frac{\left| {H}_{{\square }_{j}} \cap  {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right| }{M} \leq  {2\tau }$。根据切尔诺夫边界（完整版[20]中的引理2），在这种情况下，我们至少有$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$的概率得到$\frac{\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathcal{D}}_{1}}^{L}\right| }{\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathcal{D}}_{1}}^{B}\right|  + \left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathcal{D}}_{1}}^{L}\right| } \leq  {4\tau }$。解关于$\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{B}\right|$的不等式，可得$\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{B}\right|  \geq$$\frac{1 - {4\tau }}{4\tau }\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|  \geq  \frac{1}{8\tau }\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|$，因为$\tau  < \frac{1}{8}$。因此，$\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{B}\right|  \geq  2\left| X\right| {\left( {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\right) }^{-{d}_{u} - 1}\log \left( N\right)  \cdot  \left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|$。

Next,we find an upper bound for $\frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h < j}}\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{h}}^{L}\right|$ . Because of sorting in ascending order of $\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|$ , we have $\frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h < j}}\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{h}}^{L}\right|  \leq  \frac{j}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|  \leq  \frac{\left| L\right| }{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|  \leq  \left| X\right| {\left( {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\right) }^{-{d}_{u} - 1}\log \left( N\right)  \cdot  \left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|$ .

接下来，我们寻找$\frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h < j}}\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{h}}^{L}\right|$的上界。由于按$\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|$升序排序，故有$\frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h < j}}\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{h}}^{L}\right|  \leq  \frac{j}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|  \leq  \frac{\left| L\right| }{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|  \leq  \left| X\right| {\left( {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\right) }^{-{d}_{u} - 1}\log \left( N\right)  \cdot  \left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|$。

Hence, we conclude that

由此我们得出结论：

$$
\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{B}\right|  - \frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h < j}}\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|  \geq  2\left| X\right| {\left( {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\right) }^{-{d}_{u} - 1}\log \left( N\right) \left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|  - \left| X\right| {\left( {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\right) }^{-{d}_{u} - 1}\log \left( N\right) \left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right|  \geq  \frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\left| {\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{j}}^{L}\right| .
$$

For each point $p \in  {\bar{P}}_{u}$ ,let ${\square }_{i\left( p\right) } \in  L$ be the cell in $L$ such that $p \in  {\square }_{i\left( p\right) }$ and $p$ charges $\frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}$ points in ${\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{i\left( p\right) }}^{B}$ ,as shown in Lemma 3.7. Let ${t}_{{j}_{1}\left( p\right) },\ldots ,{t}_{{j}_{1/{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\left( p\right) }$ be these points in ${\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{i\left( p\right) }}^{B}$ .

对于每个点$p \in  {\bar{P}}_{u}$，令${\square }_{i\left( p\right) } \in  L$为$L$中的单元，使得$p \in  {\square }_{i\left( p\right) }$且$p$在${\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{i\left( p\right) }}^{B}$中计费$\frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}$个点，如引理3.7所示。设${t}_{{j}_{1}\left( p\right) },\ldots ,{t}_{{j}_{1/{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\left( p\right) }$为${\mathcal{P}}_{{\square }_{i\left( p\right) }}^{B}$中的这些点。

Next,we show that the $k$ -median error with respect to the heavy points ${P}_{u}$ approximates the $k$ -median error of all tuples in ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$ .

接下来，我们证明关于重点${P}_{u}$的$k$-中位数误差可近似表示${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$中所有元组的$k$-中位数误差。

LEMMA 3.8. Let $Y$ be an arbitrary set of $k$ points in ${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$ . It holds that ${v}_{Y}\left( {\bar{P}}_{u}\right)  \leq  {\varepsilon }^{\prime }{v}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  +$ ${\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ and ${\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + 4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$ with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ .

引理3.8. 设$Y$为${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$中任意$k$个点的集合，在概率至少为$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$时，有${v}_{Y}\left( {\bar{P}}_{u}\right)  \leq  {\varepsilon }^{\prime }{v}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  +$${\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$及${\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + 4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$成立。

Proof. We start showing the first inequality ${\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {\bar{P}}_{u}\right)  \leq  {\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  + {\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)$ . We have ${\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {\bar{P}}_{u}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  {\bar{P}}_{u}}}\phi \left( {p,Y}\right)$ . For a point $p \in  {\bar{P}}_{u}$ and each $h \leq  \frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}$ ,by triangle inequality,we have

证明. 首先证明第一个不等式${\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {\bar{P}}_{u}\right)  \leq  {\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  + {\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)$。已知${\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {\bar{P}}_{u}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  {\bar{P}}_{u}}}\phi \left( {p,Y}\right)$。对于点$p \in  {\bar{P}}_{u}$与每个$h \leq  \frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}$，根据三角不等式可得

$$
\phi \left( {p,Y}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) },Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {p,{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) }}\right) .
$$

Taking the sum of these $\frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}$ inequalities,we have $\phi \left( {p,Y}\right)  \leq  {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h = 1}}^{{1/{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}}\phi \left( {{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) },Y}\right)  + {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h = 1}}^{{1/{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}}\phi \left( {p,{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) }}\right)$ ,

将这些$\frac{1}{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}$个不等式求和，得到$\phi \left( {p,Y}\right)  \leq  {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h = 1}}^{{1/{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}}\phi \left( {{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) },Y}\right)  + {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h = 1}}^{{1/{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}}\phi \left( {p,{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) }}\right)$，

so we get

因此可得

$$
{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {\bar{P}}_{u}\right)  \leq  {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  {\bar{P}}_{u}}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h = 1}}^{{1/{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}}\phi \left( {{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) },Y}\right)  + {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  {\bar{P}}_{u}}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h = 1}}^{{1/{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}}\phi \left( {p,{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) }}\right) .
$$

From Lemma 3.7,we proved that any $t \in  {P}_{u}$ is charged by at most one point in ${\bar{P}}_{u}$ ,so the first term in the sum can be bounded as ${\varepsilon }^{\prime }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  {\bar{P}}_{u}}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h = 1}}^{{1/{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}}\phi \left( {{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) },Y}\right)  \leq  {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  = {\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$ . In Appendix $\mathrm{C}$ we bound the second term in the sum showing that ${\varepsilon }^{\prime }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  {\bar{P}}_{u}}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h = 1}}^{{1/{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}}\phi \left( {p,{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) }}\right)  \leq$ ${\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ . Hence,the first inequality follows.

根据引理3.7，我们证明了任何$t \in  {P}_{u}$最多被${\bar{P}}_{u}$中的一个点所标记，因此求和式中的第一项可界定为${\varepsilon }^{\prime }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  {\bar{P}}_{u}}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h = 1}}^{{1/{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}}\phi \left( {{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) },Y}\right)  \leq  {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  = {\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$。在附录$\mathrm{C}$中，我们通过证明${\varepsilon }^{\prime }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  {\bar{P}}_{u}}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h = 1}}^{{1/{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}}\phi \left( {p,{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) }}\right)  \leq$${\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$来界定求和式中的第二项。由此可得第一个不等式。

For the second inequality we have, ${\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  = {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  - {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {\bar{P}}_{u}\right)  \geq  {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  -$ ${\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ ,so ${\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{1 + {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + 4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$ .

对于第二个不等式，我们有${\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  = {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  - {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {\bar{P}}_{u}\right)  \geq  {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  -$${\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$，因此${\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{1 + {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + 4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$。

Using the inequalities in Lemma 3.8 and Lemma 3.6, we follow the proof of Lemma 3.2 and we show the next result.

运用引理3.8和引理3.6中的不等式，我们遵循引理3.2的证明过程，并得出以下结论。

LEMMA 3.9. $C$ is an $9{\varepsilon }^{\prime }$ -coreset of ${P}_{u}$ with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ .

引理3.9：$C$以至少$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$的概率构成${P}_{u}$的$9{\varepsilon }^{\prime }$-核心集。

Proof. Let $Y$ be an arbitrary set of $k$ points in ${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$ ,as we had in Lemma 3.2. From Lemma 3.6, let ${\varepsilon }_{p} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right\rbrack$ be a real number such that $w\left( p\right)  = \left( {1 + {\varepsilon }_{p}}\right) {n}_{p}$ ,for $p \in  C$ . With probability at least

证明：设$Y$为${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$中任意$k$个点的集合，如引理3.2所述。根据引理3.6，设${\varepsilon }_{p} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right\rbrack$为实数，使得对于$p \in  C$有$w\left( p\right)  = \left( {1 + {\varepsilon }_{p}}\right) {n}_{p}$。该情况发生的概率至少为

$$
1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}\text{,we have,}
$$

$$
\mathcal{E} = \left| {{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  - {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( C\right) }\right|  = \left| {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  C}}w\left( p\right) \phi \left( {p,Y}\right) }\right|  = \left| {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  C}}\left( {1 + {\varepsilon }_{p}}\right) {n}_{p}\phi \left( {p,Y}\right) }\right| 
$$

$$
 = \left| {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\left( {1 + {\varepsilon }_{\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) }}\right) \phi \left( {\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right|  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\left| {\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  - \left( {1 + {\varepsilon }_{\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) }}\right) \phi \left( {\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right| .
$$

This inequality also follows from Lemma 3.6. Indeed,each $\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right)$ has a weight that is $\left( {1 + {\varepsilon }_{\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) }}\right)$ times larger than the number of points in ${P}_{u}$ that are assigned to $\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right)$ . We have,

该不等式亦可由引理3.6推导得出。事实上，每个$\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right)$的权重是$\left( {1 + {\varepsilon }_{\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) }}\right)$倍于分配给$\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right)$的${P}_{u}$中的点数。我们有，

$$
\mathcal{E} \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\left| {\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  - \left( {1 + {\varepsilon }_{\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) }}\right) \phi \left( {\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right|  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\left| {\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  - \phi \left( {\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right|  + 4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\phi \left( {\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) ,Y}\right) 
$$

$$
 \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\phi \left( {t,\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) }\right)  + 4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right) .
$$

Following the proof of Lemma 3.2 we have $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\phi \left( {t,\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  {\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ . From Lemma 3.8 we get ${\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + 4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$ . We conclude that $\mathcal{E} \leq  \left( {5{\varepsilon }^{\prime } + 4{\left( {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\right) }^{2}}\right) {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  \leq  9{\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$ .

依据引理3.2的证明过程可得$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\phi \left( {t,\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  {\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$。由引理3.8可得${\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + 4{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$。由此我们得出结论：$\mathcal{E} \leq  \left( {5{\varepsilon }^{\prime } + 4{\left( {\varepsilon }^{\prime }\right) }^{2}}\right) {\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  \leq  9{\varepsilon }^{\prime }{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$。

Form Lemma 3.9, we conclude to the main result.

$$
 \leq  \left( {1 + {34}{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  = \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) .

The proof using the DkMedian ${\mathrm{{Alg}}}_{\gamma }$ algorithm is shown in Appendix C.

采用DkMedian${\mathrm{{Alg}}}_{\gamma }$算法的证明详见附录C。

Running time. The total number of cells that the algorithm processes is $O\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log N}\right)$ . In each cell,we take $M = O\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u} - 3}{\log }^{2}N}\right)$ samples. Using Lemma 2.1,for each cell we spend $O\left( {N + M\log N}\right)$ time to get the set of samples. For each sample,we check whether it belongs to a heavy cell in $B$ . This can be checked trivially in $O\left( \left| B\right| \right)  = O\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log N}\right)$ time,or in $O\left( {{\log }^{{d}_{u}}\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log N}\right) }\right)$ time using a dynamic geometric data structure for stabbing queries [5, 51]. Finally,the standard algorithm for $k$ -median clustering on $O\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log N}\right)$ points takes $O\left( {{T}_{\gamma }^{\text{med }}\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log N}\right) }\right)$ time. The overall time of the algorithm is

运行时间。算法处理的单元总数为$O\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log N}\right)$。每个单元中我们采集$M = O\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u} - 3}{\log }^{2}N}\right)$个样本。根据引理2.1，每个单元耗时$O\left( {N + M\log N}\right)$获取样本集。对每个样本，需检测是否属于$B$中的密集单元——可通过$O\left( \left| B\right| \right)  = O\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log N}\right)$时间简单判定，或采用动态几何数据结构进行穿刺查询[5,51]以$O\left( {{\log }^{{d}_{u}}\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log N}\right) }\right)$时间完成。最后，对$O\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log N}\right)$个点进行$k$-中位数聚类的标准算法耗时$O\left( {{T}_{\gamma }^{\text{med }}\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log N}\right) }\right)$。算法总时间为

$$
O\left( {N\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log \left( N\right)  + {\left| X\right| }^{2}{\varepsilon }^{-2{d}_{u} - 3}{\log }^{3}\left( N\right) \min \left\{  {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log \left( N\right) ,{\log }^{{d}_{u}}\left( \left| X\right| \right) }\right\}   + {T}_{\gamma }^{\mathrm{{med}}}\left( {\left| X\right| {\varepsilon }^{-{d}_{u}}\log N}\right) }\right) .
$$

THEOREM 3.11. Let $\mathrm{D}$ be a database instance with $N$ tuples, $q$ be an acyclic join query over a set of attributes $\mathrm{A}$ ,and ${\mathrm{A}}_{u} \subseteq  \mathrm{A}$ . Given a set $X \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,a constant $\alpha$ such that ${v}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq$ $\alpha {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ ,and a constant parameter $\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,there exists an algorithm that computes a set $\mathcal{S} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ of $k$ points and a number ${r}_{u}$ in $O\left( {\left| X\right| N\log N + {\left| X\right| }^{2}{\log }^{3}\left( N\right) \min \left\{  {{\log }^{{d}_{u}}\left( \left| X\right| \right) ,\left| X\right| \log N}\right\}   + }\right.$ ${T}_{\gamma }^{\text{med }}\left( {\left| X\right| \log N}\right)$ such that ${v}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {v}_{\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ ,with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ . There also exists an algorithm that computes a set $\mathcal{S} \subseteq  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ of $k$ points and a number ${r}_{u}$ with the same running time,such that ${v}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {v}_{{\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ ,with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ .

定理3.11. 设$\mathrm{D}$为包含$N$个元组的数据库实例，$q$为属性集$\mathrm{A}$上的无环连接查询，且${\mathrm{A}}_{u} \subseteq  \mathrm{A}$。给定集合$X \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$、常数$\alpha$使得${v}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq$$\alpha {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$，以及常数参数$\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$，存在算法可在$O\left( {\left| X\right| N\log N + {\left| X\right| }^{2}{\log }^{3}\left( N\right) \min \left\{  {{\log }^{{d}_{u}}\left( \left| X\right| \right) ,\left| X\right| \log N}\right\}   + }\right.$${T}_{\gamma }^{\text{med }}\left( {\left| X\right| \log N}\right)$时间内计算出包含$k$个点的集合$\mathcal{S} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$和数值${r}_{u}$，使得${v}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {v}_{\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$的概率至少为$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$。另存在算法能以相同时间复杂度计算出包含$k$个点的集合$\mathcal{S} \subseteq  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$和数值${r}_{u}$，使得${v}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {v}_{{\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$的概率至少为$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$。

算法3：REL-K-MEDIAN$\left( {\mathbf{q},\mathrm{D},\mathrm{R},\varepsilon ,u}\right)$

if $u$ is a leaf node then

若$u$为叶节点则

	Let ${R}_{j}$ be a relation in $\mathbf{R}$ such that ${A}_{u} \in  {\mathbf{A}}_{j}$ ;

	令${R}_{j}$为$\mathbf{R}$中满足${A}_{u} \in  {\mathbf{A}}_{j}$的关系；

	Construct join tree $T$ of $\mathbf{q}$ with ${R}_{j}$ in the root;

	构造$\mathbf{q}$的以${R}_{j}$为根节点的连接树$T$；

	Run Yannakakis algorithm on $T$ to compute $c\left( h\right)  = \left| \left\{  {t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \mid  {\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{j}}\left( t\right)  = h}\right\}  \right| ,\forall h \in  {R}_{j}$ ;

	在$T$上运行Yannakakis算法以计算$c\left( h\right)  = \left| \left\{  {t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \mid  {\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{j}}\left( t\right)  = h}\right\}  \right| ,\forall h \in  {R}_{j}$；

	${H}_{u} = {\pi }_{{A}_{u}}\left( {R}_{j}\right) ;$

	${\mathcal{S}}_{u} = \mathrm{{GkMedian}}{\mathrm{{Alg}}}_{\gamma }\left( {H}_{u}\right) ;\;{r}_{u} = {\mathbf{v}}_{{\mathcal{S}}_{u}}\left( {H}_{u}\right) ;$

else

	Let $v$ and $z$ be the children of $u$ ;

	设 $v$ 和 $z$ 为 $u$ 的子节点；

	$X = {\mathcal{S}}_{v} \times  {\mathcal{S}}_{z};\;r = {r}_{v} + {r}_{z};$

	$\left( {{\mathcal{S}}_{u},{r}_{u}}\right)  \leftarrow  \operatorname{RelClusteringFast}\left( {\mathbf{q},\mathbf{D},{\mathbf{A}}_{u},X,\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \sqrt{2},r,\varepsilon }\right) ;$

return $\left( {{\mathcal{S}}_{u},{r}_{u}}\right)$ ;
