## 4 EFFICIENT ALGORITHMS

## 4 高效算法

We use the results from Section 3 to describe a complete algorithm for the relational $k$ -median clustering. In the previous section,we saw how we can get a $\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$ -approximation algorithm if a set $X$ along with a number $r$ such that ${\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq  O\left( 1\right) {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$ are given. Essentially, in this section we focus on computing such a set $X$ and number $r$ ,efficiently.

我们利用第3章的结论，提出关系型$k$-中位数聚类的完整算法。前文已阐明，当给定集合$X$及满足${\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq  O\left( 1\right) {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$的数值$r$时，如何获得$\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$-近似算法。本节重点在于高效计算此类集合$X$与数值$r$。

### 4.1 Acyclic queries

### 4.1 无环查询

We construct a balanced binary tree $\mathcal{T}$ such that the $j$ -th leaf node stores the $j$ -th attribute ${A}_{j} \in  \mathbf{A}$ (the order is arbitrary). For a node $u$ of $\mathcal{T}$ ,let ${\mathcal{T}}_{u}$ be the subtree of $\mathcal{T}$ rooted at $u$ . Let ${\mathrm{A}}_{u}$ be the set of attributes stored in the leaf nodes of ${\mathcal{T}}_{u}$ . For every node $u$ ,our algorithm computes a set ${\mathcal{S}}_{u}$ of cardinality $k$ and a real positive number ${r}_{u}$ such that

我们构建平衡二叉树$\mathcal{T}$，其第$j$个叶节点存储第$j$个属性${A}_{j} \in  \mathbf{A}$（顺序任意）。对于$\mathcal{T}$的节点$u$，令${\mathcal{T}}_{u}$表示以$u$为根的子树。${\mathrm{A}}_{u}$是${\mathcal{T}}_{u}$叶节点存储的属性集合。算法会为每个节点$u$计算基数$k$的集合${\mathcal{S}}_{u}$和正实数${r}_{u}$，使得

$$
{\mathbf{v}}_{{\mathcal{S}}_{u}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) , \tag{5}
$$

for the relational $k$ -median clustering. For the discrete relational $k$ -median clustering,our algorithm computes a set ${\mathcal{S}}_{u}$ of cardinality $k$ and a real positive number ${r}_{u}$ such that

针对关系型$k$-中位数聚类。离散情形下，算法计算基数$k$的集合${\mathcal{S}}_{u}$和正实数${r}_{u}$，满足

$$
{\mathbf{v}}_{{\mathcal{S}}_{u}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OP}}{\mathrm{T}}_{\text{disc }}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right) , \tag{6}
$$

The definition of ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ is the same as in the previous section: ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  = {\bar{\pi }}_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ . Similarly,we use the notation ${d}_{u} = \left| {\mathbf{A}}_{u}\right|$ . For a finite set $Y \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ and a point $t \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ let $\mathcal{N}\left( {Y,t}\right)  =$ $\arg \mathop{\min }\limits_{{y \in  Y}}\phi \left( {t,y}\right)$ ,be the nearest neighbor of $t$ in $Y$ . Recall that for any set $Y \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}},{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  =$ $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}\phi \left( {{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) ,Y}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}\sqrt{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\mathrm{A}}_{u}}}{\left( {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( t\right)  - {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( \mathcal{N}\left( Y,{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) \right) \right) \right) }^{2}}.$

${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$定义同前节：${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  = {\bar{\pi }}_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$。类似地，采用记号${d}_{u} = \left| {\mathbf{A}}_{u}\right|$。对于有限集$Y \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$和点$t \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，令$\mathcal{N}\left( {Y,t}\right)  =$$\arg \mathop{\min }\limits_{{y \in  Y}}\phi \left( {t,y}\right)$表示$t$在$Y$中的最近邻。需注意对任意集合$Y \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}},{\mathbf{v}}_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  =$$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}\phi \left( {{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) ,Y}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}\sqrt{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\mathrm{A}}_{u}}}{\left( {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( t\right)  - {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( \mathcal{N}\left( Y,{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) \right) \right) \right) }^{2}}.$

The algorithm we propose works bottom-up on tree $\mathcal{T}$ . If $u$ has children $v,z$ we use the precomputed ${\mathcal{S}}_{v},{\mathcal{S}}_{z}$ and ${r}_{v},{r}_{z}$ to compute ${\mathcal{S}}_{u}$ and ${r}_{u}$ . Algorithm. First,we run the Yannakakis algorithm [55] on $\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$ and we keep only the non-dangling tuples in $\mathbf{D}$ ,i.e.,tuples in $\mathbf{D}$ that belong to at least one join result $\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$ . For any node $u$ of $\mathcal{T}$ ,in Algorithm 3 we show the pseudocode for the geometric version of relational $k$ -median clustering,computing ${\mathcal{S}}_{u}$ and ${r}_{u}$ .

我们提出的算法采用自底向上方式处理树结构$\mathcal{T}$。若$u$存在子节点$v,z$，则利用预计算的${\mathcal{S}}_{v},{\mathcal{S}}_{z}$和${r}_{v},{r}_{z}$来推导${\mathcal{S}}_{u}$与${r}_{u}$。算法步骤：首先对$\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$执行Yannakakis算法[55]，仅保留$\mathbf{D}$中的非悬挂元组（即至少属于一个连接结果$\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$的$\mathbf{D}$元组）。对于$\mathcal{T}$中的任意节点$u$，算法3展示了关系型$k$-中值聚类几何版本的伪代码，用于计算${\mathcal{S}}_{u}$和${r}_{u}$。

Let $u$ be a leaf node where ${\mathbf{A}}_{u}$ contains one attribute ${A}_{u} \in  \mathbf{A}$ . This is the only case that we try to implicitly construct ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ as a set of $O\left( N\right)$ points in ${\mathbb{R}}^{1}$ with cardinalities (weights). More specifically,we construct the weighted set of points in ${\mathbb{R}}^{1},{H}_{u} = {\pi }_{{A}_{u}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ such that $p \in  {H}_{u}$ has weight $w\left( p\right)  = \left| \left\{  {t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \mid  {\pi }_{{A}_{u}}\left( t\right)  = p}\right\}  \right|$ . Notice that ${H}_{u}$ is a set and not a multi-set. We can compute ${H}_{u}$ and the weight function $w\left( \cdot \right)$ as follows. Let ${R}_{j}$ be an arbitrary relation from $\mathbf{R}$ that contains the attribute ${A}_{u}$ . We use the counting version of Yannakakis algorithm to count the number of times that a tuple belongs in $\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ . More specifically,we construct the join tree for $\mathbf{q}$ and choose ${R}_{j}$ as its root. Using Yannakakis algorithm,for every tuple $h$ in the root ${R}_{j}$ we compute $c\left( h\right)  = \left| \left\{  {t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \mid  {\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{j}}\left( t\right)  = h}\right\}  \right|$ . By grouping together tuples from ${R}_{j}$ with the same value on attribute ${A}_{u}$ ,we compute ${H}_{u}$ and the weight function $w\left( \cdot \right)$ . More specifically,we set ${H}_{u} = {\pi }_{{A}_{u}}\left( {R}_{j}\right)$ and for each $p \in  {H}_{u}$ ,we set $w\left( p\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h \in  {R}_{j},{\pi }_{{A}_{u}}\left( h\right)  = p}}c\left( h\right)$ . Finally,we run the standard weighted $k$ -median GkMedian ${\mathrm{{Alg}}}_{\gamma }$ (or discrete $k$ -median DkMedian ${\mathrm{{Alg}}}_{\gamma }$ ) algorithm on the weighted set ${H}_{u}$ and we get a set of $k$ centers ${\mathcal{S}}_{u}$ . We also compute ${r}_{u} = {\mathbf{v}}_{{\mathcal{S}}_{u}}\left( {H}_{u}\right)$ .

设$u$为叶节点，其中${\mathbf{A}}_{u}$包含一个属性${A}_{u} \in  \mathbf{A}$。这是唯一一种我们尝试将${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$隐式构造为${\mathbb{R}}^{1}$中带基数（权重）的$O\left( N\right)$点集的情况。具体而言，我们在${\mathbb{R}}^{1},{H}_{u} = {\pi }_{{A}_{u}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$中构造带权点集，使得$p \in  {H}_{u}$具有权重$w\left( p\right)  = \left| \left\{  {t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \mid  {\pi }_{{A}_{u}}\left( t\right)  = p}\right\}  \right|$。注意${H}_{u}$是集合而非多重集。可按如下方式计算${H}_{u}$及权重函数$w\left( \cdot \right)$：令${R}_{j}$为$\mathbf{R}$中任意包含属性${A}_{u}$的关系，采用Yannakakis算法的计数版本来统计元组在$\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$中的出现次数。具体步骤包括：为$\mathbf{q}$构建连接树并选定${R}_{j}$作为根节点，对根节点中每个元组$h$运用Yannakakis算法计算$c\left( h\right)  = \left| \left\{  {t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \mid  {\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{j}}\left( t\right)  = h}\right\}  \right|$，通过按属性${A}_{u}$值分组元组，最终得到${H}_{u}$及权重函数$w\left( \cdot \right)$。具体而言，我们设定${H}_{u} = {\pi }_{{A}_{u}}\left( {R}_{j}\right)$，并对每个$p \in  {H}_{u}$设定$w\left( p\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h \in  {R}_{j},{\pi }_{{A}_{u}}\left( h\right)  = p}}c\left( h\right)$。最后，在带权集${H}_{u}$上运行标准加权$k$-中位数算法GkMedian${\mathrm{{Alg}}}_{\gamma }$（或离散$k$-中位数算法DkMedian${\mathrm{{Alg}}}_{\gamma }$），获得$k$个中心点集${\mathcal{S}}_{u}$，同时计算${r}_{u} = {\mathbf{v}}_{{\mathcal{S}}_{u}}\left( {H}_{u}\right)$。

Next,assume that $u$ is an inner node of $\mathcal{T}$ with two children $v,z$ . The algorithm sets $r = {r}_{v} + {r}_{z}$ and $X = {\mathcal{S}}_{v} \times  {\mathcal{S}}_{z}$ . Then,we run the algorithm from Theorem 3.11 (or Theorem 3.5) using $X$ and $r$ as input and compute ${\mathcal{S}}_{u}$ and ${r}_{u}$ . More specifically,for the relational $k$ -median clustering we call RelClusteringFast $\left( {\mathbf{q},\mathbf{D},{\mathbf{A}}_{u},X,\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \sqrt{2},r,\varepsilon }\right)$ ,while for the discrete relational $k$ -median clustering we call RelClusteringFast $\left( {q,\mathrm{D},{\mathrm{A}}_{u},X,2\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \sqrt{2},r,\varepsilon }\right)$ . Let $\rho$ be the root of $\mathcal{T}$ . We return the set $\mathcal{S} = {\mathcal{S}}_{\rho }.$

接下来，假设$u$是$\mathcal{T}$的一个内部节点，拥有两个子节点$v,z$。算法设置$r = {r}_{v} + {r}_{z}$和$X = {\mathcal{S}}_{v} \times  {\mathcal{S}}_{z}$。然后，我们以$X$和$r$作为输入，运行定理3.11（或定理3.5）中的算法，计算${\mathcal{S}}_{u}$和${r}_{u}$。具体而言，对于关系型$k$-中值聚类，我们调用RelClusteringFast$\left( {\mathbf{q},\mathbf{D},{\mathbf{A}}_{u},X,\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \sqrt{2},r,\varepsilon }\right)$；而对于离散关系型$k$-中值聚类，则调用RelClusteringFast$\left( {q,\mathrm{D},{\mathrm{A}}_{u},X,2\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \sqrt{2},r,\varepsilon }\right)$。设$\rho$为$\mathcal{T}$的根节点，最终返回集合$\mathcal{S} = {\mathcal{S}}_{\rho }.$

Correctness. As we explained above,for the leaf nodes,the algorithm is simple. Let $u$ be an intermediate node and let $v$ and $z$ be the two child nodes of $u$ . Assuming that ${\mathcal{S}}_{v},{r}_{v}$ and ${\mathcal{S}}_{z},{r}_{z}$ satisfy the Equation (5) (resp. Equation (6) for the discrete relational $k$ -median),we show that ${\mathcal{S}}_{u}$ and ${r}_{u}$ satisfy Equation (5) (resp. Equation (6) for the discrete relational $k$ -median). If we prove that ${\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \alpha {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ ,then the correctness follows from Theorem 3.11. The full proof of the next lemma can be found in Appendix D.

正确性验证。如前所述，对于叶节点，算法处理较为简单。设$u$为中间节点，$v$和$z$是其两个子节点。假设${\mathcal{S}}_{v},{r}_{v}$和${\mathcal{S}}_{z},{r}_{z}$满足方程(5)（对于离散关系型$k$-中值则为方程(6)），我们证明${\mathcal{S}}_{u}$和${r}_{u}$同样满足方程(5)（或离散关系型$k$-中值的方程(6)）。若能证明${\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \alpha {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$，则根据定理3.11可推出算法正确性。下一引理的完整证明见附录D。

LEMMA 4.1. If ${\mathrm{{GkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$ is used,then ${v}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \alpha {v}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ ,for $\alpha  = (1 +$ $\varepsilon )\gamma \sqrt{2}$ . If ${\mathrm{{DkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$ is used,then ${\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \alpha {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ ,for $\alpha  = 2\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \sqrt{2}$ .

引理4.1。若采用${\mathrm{{GkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$，则对于$\alpha  = (1 +$$\varepsilon )\gamma \sqrt{2}$有${v}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \alpha {v}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$；若采用${\mathrm{{DkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$，则对于$\alpha  = 2\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \sqrt{2}$有${\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \alpha {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$。

Proof. We focus on the case where ${\mathrm{{GkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$ is used. Let ${O}_{v} = {\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( {\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\right)$ ,and ${O}_{z} = {\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( {\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\right)$ . We define $O = {O}_{v} \times  {O}_{z}$ . Notice that $\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \subseteq  O$ so ${\mathbf{v}}_{O}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq$ ${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ . We have,

证明。我们重点考察使用${\mathrm{{GkMedianAlg}}}_{\gamma }$的情形。设${O}_{v} = {\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( {\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\right)$，且${O}_{z} = {\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( {\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\right)$。定义$O = {O}_{v} \times  {O}_{z}$。注意到$\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \subseteq  O$，故${\mathbf{v}}_{O}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq$${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$。由此可得：

$$
{\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}\begin{Vmatrix}{{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( {X,{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) }\right) }\end{Vmatrix}
$$

$$
 = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }}\sqrt{{\begin{Vmatrix}{\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right)  - {\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{v}}\left( \mathcal{N}\left( X,{\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) \right) \right) \end{Vmatrix}}^{2} + {\begin{Vmatrix}{\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{z}}\left( t\right)  - {\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{z}}\left( \mathcal{N}\left( X,{\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) \right) \right) \end{Vmatrix}}^{2}}
$$

$$
 \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}\begin{Vmatrix}{{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( {{\mathcal{S}}_{v},{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right) }\right) }\end{Vmatrix} + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}\begin{Vmatrix}{{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( {{\mathcal{S}}_{z},{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( t\right) }\right) }\end{Vmatrix} \leq  {r}_{v} + {r}_{z}
$$

$$
 \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  q\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}\begin{Vmatrix}{{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( {\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{v}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) ,{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right) }\right) }\end{Vmatrix} + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  q\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}\begin{Vmatrix}{{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( {\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{z}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) ,{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( t\right) }\right) }\end{Vmatrix}}\right) 
$$

$$
 \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \sqrt{2}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }}\sqrt{{\begin{Vmatrix}{\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right)  - {\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{v}}\left( \mathcal{N}\left( \mathbf{O},{\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) \right) \right) \end{Vmatrix}}^{2} + {\begin{Vmatrix}{\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{z}}\left( t\right)  - {\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{z}}\left( \mathcal{N}\left( \mathbf{O},{\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) \right) \right) \end{Vmatrix}}^{2}}
$$

$$
 = \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \sqrt{2}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}\begin{Vmatrix}{{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( {O,{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) }\right) }\end{Vmatrix} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \sqrt{2} \cdot  {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) .
$$

Similarly,if DkMedian ${\mathrm{{Alg}}}_{\gamma }$ is used,we have ${\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  2\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \sqrt{2} \cdot  {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ .

类似地，若采用DkMedian${\mathrm{{Alg}}}_{\gamma }$算法，则有${\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  2\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \sqrt{2} \cdot  {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$。

Putting everything together, we conclude with the next theorem.

综合上述结果，我们得出以下定理。

THEOREM 4.2. Given an acyclic join query $\mathbf{q}$ ,a database instance $\mathbf{D}$ ,a parameter $k$ ,and a constant parameter $\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,there exists an algorithm that computes a set $\mathcal{S} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ of $k$ points such that ${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ ,with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ . The running time of the algorithm is $O\left( {N{k}^{2}\log \left( N\right)  + {k}^{4}{\log }^{3}\left( N\right) \min \left\{  {{\log }^{d}\left( k\right) ,{k}^{2}\log N}\right\}   + {T}_{Y}^{\text{med }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$ . Furthermore, there exists an algorithm that computes a set ${\mathcal{S}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ of $k$ points in the same time such that ${\mathbf{v}}_{{\mathcal{S}}^{\prime }}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{{\operatorname{OPT}}_{\text{disc }}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ ,with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ .

定理4.2 给定无环连接查询$\mathbf{q}$、数据库实例$\mathbf{D}$、参数$k$和常数参数$\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$，存在算法可计算包含$k$个点的集合$\mathcal{S} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，使得${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$的概率不低于$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$。该算法运行时间为$O\left( {N{k}^{2}\log \left( N\right)  + {k}^{4}{\log }^{3}\left( N\right) \min \left\{  {{\log }^{d}\left( k\right) ,{k}^{2}\log N}\right\}   + {T}_{Y}^{\text{med }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$。此外，存在另一算法可在相同时间内计算包含$k$个点的集合${\mathcal{S}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$，使得${\mathbf{v}}_{{\mathcal{S}}^{\prime }}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{{\operatorname{OPT}}_{\text{disc }}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$的概率不低于$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$。

We extend the result of Theorem 4.3 for the relational $k$ -means clustering in Appendix A. Using Theorem 3.5 instead of Theorem 3.11 we can get a deterministic algorithm for the relational $k$ - median clustering problem with the same approximation guarantees that runs in $O\left( {{k}^{{2d} + 2}N{\log }^{d + 2}N + }\right.$ $\left. {{T}_{\gamma }^{\text{med }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$ time.

我们将定理4.3的结果扩展到附录A中的关系型$k$均值聚类问题。通过使用定理3.5替代定理3.11，可获得确定性算法用于关系型$k$中位数聚类问题，其近似保证相同且运行时间为$O\left( {{k}^{{2d} + 2}N{\log }^{d + 2}N + }\right.$$\left. {{T}_{\gamma }^{\text{med }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$。

### 4.2 Cyclic queries

### 4.2 循环查询

We use the notion of fractional hypertree width [28] and use a standard procedure to extend our algorithms to every (cyclic) join query $\mathbf{q}$ . For a cyclic join query $\mathbf{q}$ ,we convert it to an equivalent acyclic query such that each relation is the result of a (possibly cyclic) join query with fractional edge cover at most $\operatorname{fhw}\left( \mathbf{q}\right)$ . We evaluate the (possibly cyclic) queries to derive the new relations and then apply the algorithm from Section 4.1 on the new acyclic query. Since it is a typical method in database theory, we give the details in Appendix E.

采用分数超树宽度概念[28]，通过标准流程将算法扩展至任意（循环）连接查询$\mathbf{q}$。对于循环连接查询$\mathbf{q}$，将其转换为等价无环查询，其中每个关系都是分数边覆盖最多为$\operatorname{fhw}\left( \mathbf{q}\right)$的（可能循环）连接查询结果。通过执行（可能循环的）查询生成新关系后，对新无环查询应用第4.1节的算法。鉴于这是数据库理论的典型方法，细节详见附录E。

THEOREM 4.3. Given a join query $\mathbf{q}$ ,a database instance $\mathbf{D}$ ,a parameter $k$ ,and a constant parameter $\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,there exists an algorithm that computes a set $\mathcal{S} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ of $k$ points such that ${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ ,with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ . The running time of the algorithm is $O\left( {{N}^{\text{fhw }}{k}^{2}\log \left( N\right)  + {k}^{4}{\log }^{3}\left( N\right) \min \left\{  {{\log }^{d}\left( k\right) ,{k}^{2}\log N}\right\}   + {T}_{Y}^{\text{med }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$ . Furthermore, there exists an algorithm that computes a set ${\mathcal{S}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ of $k$ points in the same time such that ${\mathbf{v}}_{{\mathcal{S}}^{\prime }}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OP}}{\mathrm{T}}_{\text{disc }}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ ,with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ . The same results hold for the relational $k$ -means clustering problem.

定理4.3. 给定一个连接查询$\mathbf{q}$、数据库实例$\mathbf{D}$、参数$k$和常数参数$\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$，存在一个算法可计算包含$k$个点的集合$\mathcal{S} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，使得${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$的概率至少为$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$。该算法运行时间为$O\left( {{N}^{\text{fhw }}{k}^{2}\log \left( N\right)  + {k}^{4}{\log }^{3}\left( N\right) \min \left\{  {{\log }^{d}\left( k\right) ,{k}^{2}\log N}\right\}   + {T}_{Y}^{\text{med }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$。此外，存在另一个算法可在相同时间内计算包含$k$个点的集合${\mathcal{S}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$，使得${\mathbf{v}}_{{\mathcal{S}}^{\prime }}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OP}}{\mathrm{T}}_{\text{disc }}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$的概率至少为$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$。该结果同样适用于关系型$k$-均值聚类问题。

## 5 CONCLUSION

## 5 结论

In this paper we propose improved approximation algorithms for the relational $k$ -median and $k$ -means clustering. There are multiple interesting open problems derived from this work. It is interesting to check whether our geometric algorithms can be extended other clustering objective functions such as the sum of radii clustering. It is also interesting to use the ideas proposed in this paper to design clustering algorithms on the results of more complex queries such as conjunctive queries with inequalities or conjunctive queries with negation. Finally, someone can study relational clustering under different distance functions such as the Hamming, Jaccard or the cosine distance.

本文提出了改进的关系型$k$-中位数与$k$-均值聚类近似算法。本研究衍生出若干值得探索的开放性问题：我们的几何算法能否扩展至其他聚类目标函数（如半径和聚类）；能否运用本文思路为更复杂查询结果（含不等式或否定的连接查询）设计聚类算法；以及研究基于汉明距离、杰卡德距离或余弦距离等不同度量函数的关系型聚类。

## A EFFICIENT ALGORITHMS FOR RELATIONAL $k$ -MEANS CLUSTERING

## 附录A 关系型$k$-均值聚类高效算法

### A.1 Coreset for $k$ -means

### A.1 $k$-均值核心集

In this section,we focus on the relational $k$ -means clustering. The algorithm is similar with the algorithm described in section 3. However, there are changes in the analysis of the algorithms that need to be addressed. For simplicity,we focus on the (geometric) relational $k$ -means clustering. All the results are straightforwardly extended to the discrete version using ${\mathrm{{DkMeansAlg}}}_{\gamma }$ instead of ${\mathrm{{GkMeansAlg}}}_{Y}$ and using the fact that ${\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( Y\right) }\left( Y\right)  \leq  {\mu }_{{\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}\left( Y\right) }\left( Y\right)  \leq  4{\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( Y\right) }\left( Y\right)$ ,for any set of tuples $Y$ ,equivalently to the relational $k$ -median clustering problem in Sections 3,4.

本节聚焦关系型$k$-均值聚类。该算法与第3节所述算法类似，但需调整算法分析部分。为简化说明，我们重点讨论（几何）关系型$k$-均值聚类。通过将${\mathrm{{DkMeansAlg}}}_{\gamma }$替代${\mathrm{{GkMeansAlg}}}_{Y}$，并利用${\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( Y\right) }\left( Y\right)  \leq  {\mu }_{{\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}\left( Y\right) }\left( Y\right)  \leq  4{\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( Y\right) }\left( Y\right)$的性质（对任意元组集$Y$），所有结果均可直接推广至离散版本，这与第3、4节中关系型$k$-中位数聚类问题的处理方式一致。

We keep the main notation as it is in section 3 . We change the definition of the set $X$ to be a set of points in ${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$ such that ${\mu }_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \alpha  \cdot  {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ ,where $\alpha  > 1$ is a constant. Notice that

我们保持第3节中的主要符号不变。将集合$X$的定义改为${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$中满足${\mu }_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \alpha  \cdot  {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$的点集，其中$\alpha  > 1$为常数。注意

$$
{\mu }_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }}{\phi }^{2}\left( {{\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) ,X}\right) .
$$

We also assume that $r$ is a real number such that ${\mu }_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq  \alpha  \cdot  {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ . In fact,we can also assume that $\frac{r}{1 + \varepsilon } \leq  {\mu }_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ . Note that again we do not assume anything about the size of $X$ ,and in the next section,we show that we can always consider $\left| X\right|  = O\left( {k}^{2}\right)$ . Again, we assume that at first,the set $X$ and the number $r$ are given as input,and later we describe the algorithm to efficiently construct $X$ and $r$ . Similarly to the relational $k$ -median clustering,we propose two algorithms, one slower deterministic and one faster randomized. The running times of the algorithms are the same as the running times of the algorithms for the relational $k$ -median clustering.

我们还假设$r$是满足${\mu }_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  r \leq  \alpha  \cdot  {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$的实数。实际上，也可以假设$\frac{r}{1 + \varepsilon } \leq  {\mu }_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$。需再次强调的是，我们没有对$X$的规模做任何假设，下一节将证明始终可以考虑$\left| X\right|  = O\left( {k}^{2}\right)$。初始时，我们假设集合$X$和数值$r$是给定的输入参数，后续将描述高效构建$X$和$r$的算法。类似于关系型$k$-中值聚类，我们提出两种算法：一种是较慢的确定性算法，另一种是更快的随机算法。这些算法的运行时间与关系型$k$-中值聚类算法相同。

### A.2 Deterministic algorithm for k-means

### A.2 k均值确定性算法

We set $\Phi  = \sqrt{\frac{r}{\alpha n}}$ to be a lower bound estimate of the average mean radius $\sqrt{\frac{{\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }{n}}$ ,and keep all other parameters as described for the $k$ -median coreset in section 3.1. Then we construct the same exponential grids and follow the exact same algorithms to get the coreset $C$ . Then,we run the standard algorithm for the weighted $k$ -means problem on $\mathcal{C},{\mathrm{{GkMeansAlg}}}_{\gamma }\left( \mathcal{C}\right)$ ,to get a set of $k$ centers $\mathcal{S}$ . We return the set of centers $\mathcal{S}$ . Furthermore,we set and return ${r}_{u} = \frac{1}{1 - \varepsilon }{\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( \mathcal{C}\right)$ .

将$\Phi  = \sqrt{\frac{r}{\alpha n}}$设为平均半径$\sqrt{\frac{{\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }{n}}$的下界估计值，其余参数保持与第3.1节$k$-中值核心集相同。随后构建相同的指数网格，执行完全相同的算法得到核心集$C$。接着在$\mathcal{C},{\mathrm{{GkMeansAlg}}}_{\gamma }\left( \mathcal{C}\right)$上运行加权$k$-均值问题的标准算法，获得$k$个中心点$\mathcal{S}$。最终返回中心点集$\mathcal{S}$，同时设置并返回${r}_{u} = \frac{1}{1 - \varepsilon }{\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( \mathcal{C}\right)$。

Correctness. We conduct a correctness analysis by proving analogous versions of the lemmas established for the $k$ -median clustering,but this time for the $k$ -means clustering.

正确性验证。通过证明与$k$-中值聚类类似的引理版本（本次针对$k$-均值聚类）来进行正确性分析。

For any tuple $t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ ,let ${x}_{i} \in  X$ be the center that is closest to $t$ . We have that $\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i}}\right)  \leq  {\alpha n\Phi }$ . So, the following lemma is correct by the same argument as in lemma 3.1.

对于任意元组$t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$，令${x}_{i} \in  X$表示距离$t$最近的质心。根据$\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i}}\right)  \leq  {\alpha n\Phi }$可知，通过引理3.1的相同论证可证下述引理成立。

LEMMA A.1. Every tuple $t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$ is assigned to one point in $C$ . Furthermore,the number of tuples in ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ that are assigned to a point $s \in  C$ is $w\left( s\right)$ .

引理A.1. 每个元组$t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$都被映射到$C$中的一个点。此外，${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$中被映射到点$s \in  C$的元组数量为$w\left( s\right)$。

Any point $t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ is assigned to one point in $\mathcal{C}$ ,so we can define ${x}_{i\left( t\right) }$ and $\sigma \left( t\right)$ as before.

任意点$t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$都被映射到$\mathcal{C}$中的某个点，因此我们可以如前所述定义${x}_{i\left( t\right) }$和$\sigma \left( t\right)$。

LEMMA A.2. $C$ is a $k$ -means $\varepsilon$ -coreset for ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$ .

引理A.2. $C$是${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$的$k$-均值$\varepsilon$-核心集。

Proof. Let $Y$ be an arbitrary set of $k$ points in ${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$ . The error is defined as

证明. 设$Y$为${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$中任意$k$个点的集合。误差定义为

$$
\mathcal{E} = \left| {{\mu }_{Y}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  - {\mu }_{Y}\left( \mathcal{C}\right) }\right|  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }}\left| {{\phi }^{2}\left( {t,Y}\right)  - {\phi }^{2}\left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right| 
$$

$$
 = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}\left| {\left( {\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  - \phi \left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right) \left( {\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right) }\right| .
$$

By the triangle inequality, $\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)$ and $\phi \left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)$ . Hence, $\left| {\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  - \phi \left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right|  \leq  \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)$ . We have,

根据三角不等式，$\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)$且$\phi \left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)$。因此，$\left| {\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  - \phi \left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right|  \leq  \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)$。我们有

$$
\mathcal{E} \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }}\left| {\left( {\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  - \phi \left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right) \left( {\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {\sigma \left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right) }\right|  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }}\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right) ({2\phi }\left( {t,Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right) .
$$

Now we divide the points of ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ into three cases. Let ${P}_{R} = \left\{  {t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \mid  \phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  \leq  \Phi  \land  }\right.$ $\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  \leq  \Phi \}$ ,and ${P}_{X} = \left\{  {t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \smallsetminus  {P}_{R} \mid  \phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right) }\right\}$ ,and ${P}_{Y} = \left\{  {t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \smallsetminus  {P}_{R} \mid  \phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  < }\right.$ $\phi \left( {t,Y}\right) \}$ . Note that we do not actually construct these sets,instead we only analyze the error induced by these three types of points separately. It is straightforward to see that ${P}_{R} \cup  {P}_{X} \cup  {P}_{Y} =$ ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ and ${P}_{R} \cap  {P}_{X} = \varnothing ,{P}_{R} \cap  {P}_{Y} = \varnothing ,{P}_{X} \cap  {P}_{Y} = \varnothing$ .

现将${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$的点分为三种情况。设${P}_{R} = \left\{  {t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)  \mid  \phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  \leq  \Phi  \land  }\right.$$\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  \leq  \Phi \}$，且${P}_{X} = \left\{  {t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \smallsetminus  {P}_{R} \mid  \phi \left( {t,Y}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right) }\right\}$，且${P}_{Y} = \left\{  {t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \smallsetminus  {P}_{R} \mid  \phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  < }\right.$$\phi \left( {t,Y}\right) \}$。注意我们并不实际构造这些集合，而是分别分析这三类点引起的误差。显然${P}_{R} \cup  {P}_{X} \cup  {P}_{Y} =$${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$且${P}_{R} \cap  {P}_{X} = \varnothing ,{P}_{R} \cap  {P}_{Y} = \varnothing ,{P}_{X} \cap  {P}_{Y} = \varnothing$。

For a point $t \in  {P}_{R}$ ,since $\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  \leq  \Phi$ ,by the construction of the exponential grid,we have that $\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \operatorname{diam}\left( {\square }_{t}\right)  \leq  \frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\Phi$ ,hence

对于点$t \in  {P}_{R}$，由于$\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  \leq  \Phi$，根据指数网格的构造原理，我们得出$\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \operatorname{diam}\left( {\square }_{t}\right)  \leq  \frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\Phi$，因此

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{R}}}\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right) \left( {{2\phi }\left( {t,Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{R}}}\frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\Phi \left( {{2\Phi } + \frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\Phi }\right)  \leq  \frac{3\varepsilon }{10\alpha }n{\Phi }^{2} \leq  \frac{3\varepsilon }{10}{\mu }_{{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) .}\right. 
$$

By the definition of the set ${P}_{X}$ ,we have $\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  > \Phi$ ,hence,as shown in the proof of lemma 3.2, $\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)$ ,and $\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \frac{4\varepsilon }{10\alpha }}\right) \phi \left( {t,X}\right)$ . Therefore,

根据集合${P}_{X}$的定义，我们有$\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  > \Phi$，因此如引理3.2证明所示，$\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)$且$\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \frac{4\varepsilon }{10\alpha }}\right) \phi \left( {t,X}\right)$。故得

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{X}}}\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right) \left( {{2\phi }\left( {t,Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{X}}}\frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right) \left( {{2\phi }\left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  + \frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right) }\right) }\right. 
$$

$$
 \leq  \frac{3\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{X}}}{\phi }^{2}\left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  \leq  \frac{3\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{X}}}\left( {\left( {1 + \frac{4\varepsilon }{10\alpha }}\right) {\phi }^{2}\left( {t,X}\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{12\varepsilon }{10\alpha }{\mu }_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{12\varepsilon }{10}{\mu }_{{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) .
$$

For the last case we have $\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)$ . It holds because: If $\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  \leq  \Phi$ ,then $\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\Phi  \leq  \frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)$ . If $\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  > \Phi$ then $\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  \leq  \frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)$ . Hence, we have,

最后一种情形下存在$\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)$。其成立依据为：若$\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  \leq  \Phi$，则$\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\Phi  \leq  \frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)$；若$\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  > \Phi$则$\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\phi \left( {t,{x}_{i\left( t\right) }}\right)  \leq  \frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\phi \left( {t,Y}\right)$。由此可得

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{Y}}}\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right) \left( {{2\phi }\left( {t,Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{Y}}}\frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\phi \left( {t,Y}\right) \left( {{2\phi }\left( {t,Y}\right)  + \frac{\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\phi \left( {t,Y}\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{3\varepsilon }{10\alpha }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{Y}}}{\phi }^{2}\left( {t,Y}\right) }\right. 
$$

$$
 \leq  \frac{3\varepsilon }{10\alpha }{\mu }_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) .
$$

Finally, we bound the error,

最终我们对误差进行约束

$$
\mathcal{E} \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right) ({2\phi }\left( {t,Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right) 
$$

$$
 \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{R}}}\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right) \left( {{2\phi }\left( {t,Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right)  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{X}}}\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right) \left( {{2\phi }\left( {t,Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right) }\right. }\right. 
$$

$$
 + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{Y}}}\phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right) ({2\phi }\left( {t,Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {t,\sigma \left( t\right) }\right) 
$$

$$
 \leq  \frac{3\varepsilon }{10}{\mu }_{{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  + \frac{12\varepsilon }{10}{\mu }_{{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  + \frac{3\varepsilon }{10\alpha }{\mu }_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{18\varepsilon }{10}{\mu }_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) .
$$

Thus,if we set $\varepsilon  \leftarrow  \varepsilon /{18}$ ,the result follows.

因此，若设定$\varepsilon  \leftarrow  \varepsilon /{18}$，即可得证结论

With the same argument as in lemma 3.3, we can prove the following lemma.

沿用引理3.3的论证方法，可证明下述引理

Lemma A.3. ${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ .

引理A.3 ${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$

As the algorithm for constructing the $k$ -means coreset is almost the same as the one for the $k$ -median’s coreset,and the only difference is in the analysis,the running time is the same and we can conclude with the following theorem.

由于构建$k$-均值核心集的算法与$k$-中位数核心集基本相同，仅分析过程存在差异，故运行时间相同，由此可得以下定理

THEOREM A.4. Let $\mathbf{D}$ be a database instance with $N$ tuples, $\mathbf{q}$ be an acyclic join query over a set of attributes $\mathbf{A}$ and ${\mathbf{A}}_{u} \subseteq  \mathbf{A}$ . Given a set $X \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,a constant $\alpha$ such that ${\mu }_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \alpha {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ , and a constant parameter $\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,there exists an algorithm that computes a set $\mathcal{S} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ of $k$ points and a number ${r}_{u}$ in $O\left( {{\left| X\right| }^{{d}_{u} + 1}N{\log }^{{d}_{u} + 2}N + {T}_{Y}^{\text{mean }}\left( {\left| X\right| \log N}\right) }\right)$ time such that ${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  (1 +$ $\varepsilon )\gamma {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ . There also exists an algorithm that computes a set $\mathcal{S} \subseteq  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ of $k$ points and a number ${r}_{u}$ with the same running time,such that ${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {4 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mu }_{{\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) \left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ .

定理A.4。设$\mathbf{D}$为包含$N$个元组的数据库实例，$\mathbf{q}$为属性集$\mathbf{A}$和${\mathbf{A}}_{u} \subseteq  \mathbf{A}$上的无环连接查询。给定集合$X \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$、常数$\alpha$满足${\mu }_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \alpha {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$，以及常数参数$\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$，存在一个算法可在$O\left( {{\left| X\right| }^{{d}_{u} + 1}N{\log }^{{d}_{u} + 2}N + {T}_{Y}^{\text{mean }}\left( {\left| X\right| \log N}\right) }\right)$时间内计算出包含$k$个点的集合$\mathcal{S} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$和数值${r}_{u}$，使得${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{q}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  (1 +$$\varepsilon )\gamma {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$。同时存在另一算法，在相同时间复杂度下计算出包含$k$个点的集合$\mathcal{S} \subseteq  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$和数值${r}_{u}$，满足${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {4 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mu }_{{\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) \left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$。

### A.3 Randomized Algorithm for k-means Clustering

### A.3 k均值聚类的随机算法

The algorithm for the $k$ -means problem is exactly the same as the one we described in section 3.2 for the $k$ -median problem. Hence,in this section,we use the same notation and prove the additional lemmas required to show that the algorithm works also for $k$ -means clustering. Note that this algorithm builds the $k$ -means version of the exponential grid as described in A.2,and then continues with the randomized counting algorithm described in section 3.2.

该$k$均值问题算法与第3.2节所述$k$中值问题算法完全相同。因此本节沿用相同符号体系，通过证明新增引理来说明该算法同样适用于$k$均值聚类。需注意该算法首先构建如A.2节所述的$k$均值版指数网格，继而延续第3.2节所述的随机计数算法流程。

Correctness. Notice that Lemmas 3.6 and 3.7 are independent of the objective function of the problem and we can directly assume they are true without proving them again in this section.

正确性。注意引理3.6和3.7独立于问题的目标函数，本节可直接沿用其结论而无需重新证明。

We prove the following lemma using the same notation as in Lemma 3.8.

我们采用与引理3.8相同的符号体系来证明下述引理。

LEMMA A.5. Let $Y$ be an arbitrary set of $k$ points in ${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$ . It holds that ${\mu }_{Y}\left( {\bar{P}}_{u}\right)  \leq  \varepsilon {\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  +$ $\varepsilon {\mu }_{Y}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ and ${\mu }_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) {\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$ with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ .

引理 A.5 设$Y$为${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$空间中任意$k$个点的集合，其满足${\mu }_{Y}\left( {\bar{P}}_{u}\right)  \leq  \varepsilon {\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  +$$\varepsilon {\mu }_{Y}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$且${\mu }_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) {\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$的概率不低于$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$。

Proof. We first start showing the first inequality. For a point $p \in  {\bar{P}}_{u}$ and each $h \leq  \frac{1}{\varepsilon }$ ,by triangle inequality, we have

证明 首先证明第一个不等式。对于点$p \in  {\bar{P}}_{u}$与每个$h \leq  \frac{1}{\varepsilon }$，根据三角不等式可得

$$
\phi \left( {p,Y}\right)  \leq  \phi \left( {{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) },Y}\right)  + \phi \left( {p,{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) }}\right) .
$$

By squaring the above inequality we get,

将上述不等式平方后得到

$$
{\phi }^{2}\left( {p,Y}\right)  \leq  2{\phi }^{2}\left( {{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) },Y}\right)  + 2{\phi }^{2}\left( {p,{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) }}\right) .
$$

Taking the sum of these $\frac{1}{\varepsilon }$ inequalities,we have,

对这$\frac{1}{\varepsilon }$个不等式求和可得

$$
{\phi }^{2}\left( {p,Y}\right)  \leq  {2\varepsilon }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h = 1}}^{{1/\varepsilon }}{\phi }^{2}\left( {{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) },Y}\right)  + {2\varepsilon }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h = 1}}^{{1/\varepsilon }}{\phi }^{2}\left( {p,{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) }}\right) .
$$

So, we have

因此我们得到

$$
{\mu }_{Y}\left( {\bar{P}}_{u}\right)  \leq  {2\varepsilon }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  {\bar{P}}_{u}}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h = 1}}^{{1/\varepsilon }}{\phi }^{2}\left( {{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) },Y}\right)  + {2\varepsilon }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  {\bar{P}}_{u}}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h = 1}}^{{1/\varepsilon }}{\phi }^{2}\left( {p,{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) }}\right) .
$$

From Lemma 3.7,we proved that any $t \in  {P}_{u}$ is assigned by at most one point in ${\bar{P}}_{u}$ ,so the first term in the sum can be bounded as

根据引理3.7，我们证明任何$t \in  {P}_{u}$最多被${\bar{P}}_{u}$中一个点所分配，故求和式中第一项可界定为

$$
{2\varepsilon }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  {\bar{P}}_{u}}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h = 1}}^{{1/\varepsilon }}{\phi }^{2}\left( {{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) },Y}\right)  \leq  {2\varepsilon }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}{\phi }^{2}\left( {t,Y}\right)  = {2\varepsilon }{\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right) .
$$

Next, we bound the second term in the sum.

接下来界定求和式中的第二项

We define a new assignment function ${\sigma }^{\prime }$ as we did in the proof of Lemma 3.8,and with the same argument we get,

如引理3.8证明中所述，定义新的分配函数${\sigma }^{\prime }$，同理可得

$$
{2\varepsilon }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  {\bar{P}}_{u}}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h = 1}}^{{1/\varepsilon }}{\phi }^{2}\left( {p,{t}_{{j}_{h}\left( p\right) }}\right)  \leq  {2\varepsilon }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}{\phi }^{2}\left( {t,{\sigma }^{\prime }\left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  {2\varepsilon }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }}{\phi }^{2}\left( {t,{\sigma }^{\prime }\left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{36\varepsilon }{10}{\mu }_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) .
$$

The last inequality holds using the arguments in the proof of Lemma A.2. Finally, by summing up the two terms and setting $\varepsilon  \leftarrow  \frac{\varepsilon }{6}$ ,the first inequality follows. Then we prove the second inequality.

最后不等式成立基于引理A.2证明中的论证。综上两项并设$\varepsilon  \leftarrow  \frac{\varepsilon }{6}$，即得第一个不等式。随后证明第二个不等式。

$$
{\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  = {\mu }_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  - {\mu }_{Y}\left( {\bar{P}}_{u}\right)  \geq  {\mu }_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  - \varepsilon {\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  - \varepsilon {\mu }_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \Leftrightarrow  {\mu }_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{1 + \varepsilon }{1 - \varepsilon }{\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right) .
$$

If we set $\varepsilon  \leftarrow  \varepsilon /3$ ,the result follows.

若设$\varepsilon  \leftarrow  \varepsilon /3$，结论得证。

LEMMA A.6. $C$ is a $k$ -means $\varepsilon$ -coreset of ${P}_{u}$ with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ .

引理A.6 $C$是以至少$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$概率成为${P}_{u}$的$k$均值$\varepsilon$核心集。

Proof. Let $Y$ be an arbitrary set of $k$ points in ${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$ . With probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ ,we have,

证明：设$Y$为${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$中任意$k$个点集，以至少$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$概率可得：

$$
\mathcal{E} = \left| {{\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  - {\mu }_{Y}\left( C\right) }\right|  = \left| {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}{\phi }^{2}\left( {t,Y}\right)  - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  C}}w\left( p\right) {\phi }^{2}\left( {p,Y}\right) }\right|  = \left| {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}{\phi }^{2}\left( {t,Y}\right)  - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  C}}\left( {1 + {\varepsilon }_{p}}\right) {n}_{p}{\phi }^{2}\left( {p,Y}\right) }\right| 
$$

$$
 = \left| {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}{\phi }^{2}\left( {t,Y}\right)  - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\left( {1 + {\varepsilon }_{\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) }}\right) {\phi }^{2}\left( {\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right|  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\left| {{\phi }^{2}\left( {t,Y}\right)  - \left( {1 + {\varepsilon }_{\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) }}\right) {\phi }^{2}\left( {\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right| .
$$

This inequality also follows from Lemma 3.6. We have,

此不等式亦可由引理3.6推出。我们有：

$$
\mathcal{E} \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\left| {{\phi }^{2}\left( {t,Y}\right)  - \left( {1 + {\varepsilon }_{\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) }}\right) {\phi }^{2}\left( {\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right|  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\left| {{\phi }^{2}\left( {t,Y}\right)  - {\phi }^{2}\left( {\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right|  + \varepsilon \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}{\phi }^{2}\left( {\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) ,Y}\right) 
$$

$$
 \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\left| {{\phi }^{2}\left( {t,Y}\right)  - {\phi }^{2}\left( {\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right|  + {2\varepsilon }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\left( {{\phi }^{2}\left( {t,\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) }\right)  + {\phi }^{2}\left( {t,Y}\right) }\right) .
$$

Using the proofs of Lemma A. 2 and Lemma A.5,we have $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\left| {{\phi }^{2}\left( {t,Y}\right)  - {\phi }^{2}\left( {\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right|  \leq  (1 +$ $\varepsilon )\frac{18\varepsilon }{10}{\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$ ,and $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}{\phi }^{2}\left( {t,\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \frac{18\varepsilon }{10}{\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$ . Thus,we have,

根据引理A.2与引理A.5的证明，可得$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}\left| {{\phi }^{2}\left( {t,Y}\right)  - {\phi }^{2}\left( {\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) ,Y}\right) }\right|  \leq  (1 +$$\varepsilon )\frac{18\varepsilon }{10}{\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$及$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  {P}_{u}}}{\phi }^{2}\left( {t,\widehat{\sigma }\left( t\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \frac{18\varepsilon }{10}{\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$。因此有：

$$
\mathcal{E} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \frac{18\varepsilon }{10}{\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  + \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \frac{{36}{\varepsilon }^{2}}{10}{\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  + {2\varepsilon }{\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  \leq  \frac{130}{10}\varepsilon {\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right) .
$$

So,if we set $\varepsilon  \leftarrow  \frac{\varepsilon }{13}$ ,the result follows.

故当设$\varepsilon  \leftarrow  \frac{\varepsilon }{13}$时，结论成立。

Finally,we show that ${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ is a good approximation of ${\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right. }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ and that ${r}_{u} =$ $\frac{1 + \varepsilon }{{\left( 1 - \varepsilon \right) }^{2}}{\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( C\right)$ is a good estimate of ${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ .

最终我们证明${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$是${\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right. }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$的良好近似，且${r}_{u} =$$\frac{1 + \varepsilon }{{\left( 1 - \varepsilon \right) }^{2}}{\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( C\right)$是${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$的有效估计。

LEMMA A.7. ${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ ,with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ .

引理A.7 ${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$，其概率不低于$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$。

Proof. From Lemma A.2,we have that for any set of $k$ points $Y$ in ${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}},\left( {1 - \varepsilon }\right) {\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  \leq  {\mu }_{Y}\left( \mathcal{C}\right)  \leq$ $\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) {\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$ ,with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ . By definition,

证明：由引理A.2可知，对于${\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}},\left( {1 - \varepsilon }\right) {\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  \leq  {\mu }_{Y}\left( \mathcal{C}\right)  \leq$$\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) {\mu }_{Y}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$中任意$k$个点集$Y$，以至少$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$概率成立。根据定义：

$$
{\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( \mathcal{C}\right)  \leq  \gamma {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( \mathcal{C}\right) }\left( \mathcal{C}\right)  \leq  \gamma {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {P}_{u}\right) }\left( \mathcal{C}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {P}_{u}\right) }\left( {P}_{u}\right) . \tag{7}
$$

The last inequality follows by the definition of the coreset for $Y = \operatorname{OPT}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$ . Since ${P}_{u} \subseteq  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$ and $\operatorname{OPT}\left( {P}_{u}\right) ,\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,it also holds that ${\mu }_{\operatorname{OPT}\left( {P}_{u}\right) }\left( {P}_{u}\right)  \leq  {\mu }_{\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ .

最后一个不等式由$Y = \operatorname{OPT}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$的核心集定义得出。由于${P}_{u} \subseteq  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$和$\operatorname{OPT}\left( {P}_{u}\right) ,\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，故${\mu }_{\operatorname{OPT}\left( {P}_{u}\right) }\left( {P}_{u}\right)  \leq  {\mu }_{\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$也成立。

From Lemma A. 5 (for $Y = \mathcal{S}$ ) we have ${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) {\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$ . Hence,

根据引理A.5（针对$Y = \mathcal{S}$）可得${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) {\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {P}_{u}\right)$。因此，

$$
{\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) {\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {P}_{u}\right)  \leq  \frac{1 + \varepsilon }{1 - \varepsilon }{\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( \mathcal{C}\right)  = {r}_{u} \leq  \frac{{\left( 1 + \varepsilon \right) }^{2}}{1 - \varepsilon }\gamma {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) .
$$

If we set $\varepsilon  \leftarrow  \varepsilon /5$ ,the result follows.

若设定$\varepsilon  \leftarrow  \varepsilon /5$，结论即成立。

Notice that there is no difference between the above algorithm and the one for $k$ -median proposed in section 3.2, and the only difference is in the correctness analysis. Therefore, the running is exactly the same, and we can directly have the following theorem.

注意上述算法与3.2节提出的$k$-中值算法并无差异，唯一区别在于正确性分析。因此运行时间完全相同，可直接得出以下定理。

THEOREM A.8. Let $\mathrm{D}$ be a database instance with $N$ tuples, $\mathbf{q}$ be an acyclic join query over a set of attributes $\mathbf{A}$ ,and ${\mathbf{A}}_{u} \subseteq  \mathbf{A}$ . Given a set $X \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ ,a constant $\alpha$ such that ${\mu }_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq$ $\alpha {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ ,and a constant parameter $\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,there exists an algorithm that computes a set $\mathcal{S} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ of $k$ points and a number ${r}_{u}$ in $O\left( {\left| X\right| N\log N + {\left| X\right| }^{2}{\log }^{3}\left( N\right) \min \left\{  {{\log }^{{d}_{u}}\left( \left| X\right| \right) ,\left| X\right| \log N}\right\}   + }\right.$ ${T}_{Y}^{\text{mean }}\left( {\left| X\right| \log N}\right) )$ such that ${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mu }_{\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ ,with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ . There also exists an algorithm that computes a set $\mathcal{S} \subseteq  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$ of $k$ points and a number ${r}_{u}$ with the same running time,such that ${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {4 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mu }_{{\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ ,with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ .

定理A.8。设$\mathrm{D}$为具有$N$个元组的数据库实例，$\mathbf{q}$为属性集$\mathbf{A}$上的无环连接查询，且${\mathbf{A}}_{u} \subseteq  \mathbf{A}$。给定集合$X \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$、常数$\alpha$使得${\mu }_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq$$\alpha {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$，以及常数参数$\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$，存在算法可计算包含$k$个点的集合$\mathcal{S} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$和数值${r}_{u}$，在$O\left( {\left| X\right| N\log N + {\left| X\right| }^{2}{\log }^{3}\left( N\right) \min \left\{  {{\log }^{{d}_{u}}\left( \left| X\right| \right) ,\left| X\right| \log N}\right\}   + }\right.$${T}_{Y}^{\text{mean }}\left( {\left| X\right| \log N}\right) )$内满足${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mu }_{\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$，其概率至少为$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$。另存在算法可在相同运行时间内计算包含$k$个点的集合$\mathcal{S} \subseteq  {\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right)$和数值${r}_{u}$，使得${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {4 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mu }_{{\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$，其概率至少为$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$。

### A.4 k-means Clustering

### A.4 k均值聚类

We use the results from the previous section to describe a complete algorithm for the relational $k$ - means clustering. As we did in section 4.1, in this section, we describe how to efficiently construct the set $X$ and the number $r$ . To do so,we construct a binary tree as described in section 4.1,and use the same notation. Our goal this time is to compute for each node $u$ ,a set ${\mathcal{S}}_{u}$ ,and a number ${r}_{u}$ ,such that,

我们利用前文结论描述关系型$k$均值聚类的完整算法。如第4.1节所述，本节将阐述如何高效构建集合$X$与数值$r$。为此，我们按4.1节方法构建二叉树并沿用相同符号体系。本次目标是为每个节点$u$计算集合${\mathcal{S}}_{u}$和数值${r}_{u}$，使得

$$
{\mu }_{{\mathcal{S}}_{u}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) . \tag{8}
$$

The algorithm is exactly the same as the algorithm for $k$ -median clustering. However,instead of running a standard $k$ -median algorithm on the leaf node,we run a standard $k$ -means algorithm, and on the intermediate nodes,we use the $k$ -means version of our coresets described in Section A.1. At last,we report $\mathcal{S} = {\mathcal{S}}_{\rho }$ ,where $\rho$ is the root of the tree.

该算法与$k$-中位数聚类算法完全相同。不同之处在于，我们不在叶节点上运行标准$k$-中位数算法，而是运行标准$k$-均值算法；在中间节点上，则采用第A.1节所述核心集的$k$-均值版本。最终输出$\mathcal{S} = {\mathcal{S}}_{\rho }$，其中$\rho$表示树的根节点。

## Correctness.

## 正确性证明

Recall that for any set $Y \subseteq  {\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$ ,

对于任意集合$Y \subseteq  {\mathbb{R}}^{{d}_{u}}$，

$$
{\mu }_{Y}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }}{\phi }^{2}\left( {{\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) ,Y}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\mathbf{A}}_{u}}}{\left( {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( t\right)  - {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( \mathcal{N}\left( Y,{\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) \right) \right) \right) }^{2}.
$$

Assuming that ${\mathcal{S}}_{v},{r}_{v}$ and ${\mathcal{S}}_{z},{r}_{z}$ satisfy Equation 8,we show how to compute ${\mathcal{S}}_{u}$ and ${r}_{u}$ that satisfy Equation 8.

假设${\mathcal{S}}_{v},{r}_{v}$和${\mathcal{S}}_{z},{r}_{z}$满足公式8，我们将演示如何计算满足公式8的${\mathcal{S}}_{u}$和${r}_{u}$。

If we prove that $X$ is a constant $\alpha$ -approximation of the $k$ -means problem on ${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ and ${\mu }_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq$ ${r}_{u} \leq  \alpha {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)$ ,then the correctness follows from Theorem A.8 (or Theorem A.4).

若能证明$X$是${\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$上$k$-均值问题的常数$\alpha$近似解，且${\mu }_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq$${r}_{u} \leq  \alpha {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)$成立，则根据定理A.8（或定理A.4）可证其正确性。

Lemma A.9. ${\mu }_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \alpha {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)$ .

引理A.9.${\mu }_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {r}_{u} \leq  \alpha {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)$

Proof. Let ${O}_{v} = {\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( {\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right) }\right)$ ,and ${O}_{z} = {\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( {\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right) }\right)$ . We define $O = {O}_{v} \times  {O}_{z}$ . Notice that $\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)  \subseteq  O$ so ${\mu }_{O}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)$ . We have,

证明。设${O}_{v} = {\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( {\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right) }\right)$，且${O}_{z} = {\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( {\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right) }\right)$。定义$O = {O}_{v} \times  {O}_{z}$。注意到$\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)  \subseteq  O$，故${\mu }_{O}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }\right)$。可得：

$$
{\mu }_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }}{\begin{Vmatrix}{\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( X,{\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) \right) \end{Vmatrix}}^{2}
$$

$$
 = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}{\begin{Vmatrix}{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right)  - {\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( \mathcal{N}\left( X,{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) \right) \right) \end{Vmatrix}}^{2} + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}{\begin{Vmatrix}{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( t\right)  - {\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( \mathcal{N}\left( X,{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) \right) \right) \end{Vmatrix}}^{2}
$$

$$
 = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}{\begin{Vmatrix}{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{v},{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right) \right) \end{Vmatrix}}^{2} + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}{\begin{Vmatrix}{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{z},{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( t\right) \right) \end{Vmatrix}}^{2}
$$

$$
 \leq  {r}_{v} + {r}_{z}
$$

$$
 \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  q\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }}{\begin{Vmatrix}{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( \mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {\mathbf{q}}_{v}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) \right) ,{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right) \right) \end{Vmatrix}}^{2} + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  q\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }}{\begin{Vmatrix}{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( \mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {\mathbf{q}}_{z}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) \right) ,{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( t\right) \right) \end{Vmatrix}}^{2}}\right) 
$$

$$
 \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  q\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}{\begin{Vmatrix}{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( {O}_{v},{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right) \right) \end{Vmatrix}}^{2} + \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  q\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}{\begin{Vmatrix}{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( {O}_{z},{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( t\right) \right) \end{Vmatrix}}^{2}
$$

$$
 = \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  q\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}\left( {{\begin{Vmatrix}{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right)  - {\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( \mathcal{N}\left( O,{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) \right) \right) \end{Vmatrix}}^{2} + {\begin{Vmatrix}{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( t\right)  - {\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( \mathcal{N}\left( O,{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) \right) \right) \end{Vmatrix}}^{2}}\right) 
$$

$$
 = \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}{\begin{Vmatrix}{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( O,{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) \right) \end{Vmatrix}}^{2} = \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma  \cdot  {\mu }_{O}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) 
$$

$$
 \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma  \cdot  {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) .
$$

The above inequalities are mostly simpler versions of the inequalities in Lemma 4.1, and they hold by the same arguments. Overall,we can set $\alpha  = \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$ which is a constant,and the result follows.

上述不等式本质上是引理4.1中不等式的简化版本，其成立依据相同论证。最终可取常数$\alpha  = \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$，结论得证。

We have that $\mathcal{S} = {\mathcal{S}}_{\rho }$ is a $\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$ approximation of the relational $k$ -median problem in $\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ .

我们得出$\mathcal{S} = {\mathcal{S}}_{\rho }$是$\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$中关系型$k$-中位数问题的$\left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma$近似解。

The algorithm is the same as the proposed algorithm for the $k$ -median problem,and it has the same time complexity. Therefore we can conclude with the following theorem.

本算法与$k$-中位数问题的解决方案相同，时间复杂度一致。因此可得出以下定理。

THEOREM A.10. Given an acyclic join query $\mathbf{q}$ ,a database instance $\mathbf{D}$ ,a parameter $k$ ,and a constant parameter $\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,there exists an algorithm that computes a set $\mathcal{S} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$ of $k$ points such that ${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ ,with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ . The running time of the algorithm is $O\left( {N{k}^{2}\log \left( N\right)  + {k}^{4}{\log }^{3}\left( N\right) \min \left\{  {{\log }^{d}\left( k\right) ,{k}^{2}\log N}\right\}   + {T}_{\gamma }^{\text{mean }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$ . Furthermore, there exists an algorithm that computes a set ${\mathcal{S}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$ of $k$ points in the same time such that ${\mu }_{{\mathcal{S}}^{\prime }}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {4 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mu }_{{\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ ,with probability at least $1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$ .

定理A.10。给定一个无环连接查询$\mathbf{q}$、数据库实例$\mathbf{D}$、参数$k$和常数参数$\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$，存在一个算法可计算由$k$个点组成的集合$\mathcal{S} \subset  {\mathbb{R}}^{d}$，使得${\mu }_{\mathcal{S}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mu }_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$的概率至少为$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$。该算法的运行时间为$O\left( {N{k}^{2}\log \left( N\right)  + {k}^{4}{\log }^{3}\left( N\right) \min \left\{  {{\log }^{d}\left( k\right) ,{k}^{2}\log N}\right\}   + {T}_{\gamma }^{\text{mean }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$。此外，存在另一个算法可在相同时间内计算由$k$个点组成的集合${\mathcal{S}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right)$，使得${\mu }_{{\mathcal{S}}^{\prime }}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {4 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mu }_{{\mathrm{{OPT}}}_{\text{disc }}\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {\mathbf{q}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$的概率同样至少为$1 - \frac{1}{{N}^{O\left( 1\right) }}$。

Using Theorem A.4, we can also get a deterministic algorithm with the same guarantees that runs in $O\left( {{k}^{{2d} + 2}N{\log }^{d + 2}N + {T}_{\gamma }^{\text{mean }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$ time.

利用定理A.4，我们还可以得到一个具有相同保证的确定性算法，其运行时间为$O\left( {{k}^{{2d} + 2}N{\log }^{d + 2}N + {T}_{\gamma }^{\text{mean }}\left( {{k}^{2}\log N}\right) }\right)$。

The first and second equalities hold by the definition of the Euclidean metric. The third inequality holds because $\sqrt{a + b} \leq  \sqrt{a} + \sqrt{b}$ ,for $a,b > 0$ .

We show the fourth inequality by proof by contradiction.
