Without loss of generality,assume that $s$ is the unique nearest neighbor. Assume that ${\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( {\mathcal{N}\left( {X,{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) }\right) }\right)  \neq  \mathcal{N}\left( {{\mathcal{S}}_{v},{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right) }\right)  \Leftrightarrow  {\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( x\right)  \neq  s$ . In fact,assume that ${\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{v}}\left( x\right)  = {s}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{S}}_{v}$ such that ${s}^{\prime } \neq  s$ ,and ${\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{z}}\left( x\right)  = \bar{s} \in  {\mathcal{S}}_{z}$ . Let ${x}^{\prime } = s \times  \bar{s} \in  X$ . By definition, $\sqrt{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\mathbf{A}}_{v}}}{\left( {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( s\right)  - {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( t\right) \right) }^{2}} < \sqrt{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\mathbf{A}}_{v}}}{\left( {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( {s}^{\prime }\right)  - {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( t\right) \right) }^{2}}$ . We have,

我们通过反证法证明第四个不等式。注意到对于任意$\bar{x} \in  X,{\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{v}}\left( \bar{x}\right)  \in  {\mathcal{S}}_{v}$，由于$X = {\mathcal{S}}_{v} \times  {\mathcal{S}}_{z}$。设$s \in  {\mathcal{S}}_{v}$为${\mathcal{S}}_{v}$中距离${\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right)$最近的点，$x \in  X$为$X$中距离${\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right)$最近的点。不失一般性，假设$s$是唯一最近邻。假设${\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( {\mathcal{N}\left( {X,{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) }\right) }\right)  \neq  \mathcal{N}\left( {{\mathcal{S}}_{v},{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right) }\right)  \Leftrightarrow  {\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( x\right)  \neq  s$。实际上，假设${\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{v}}\left( x\right)  = {s}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{S}}_{v}$使得${s}^{\prime } \neq  s$，且${\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{z}}\left( x\right)  = \bar{s} \in  {\mathcal{S}}_{z}$。令${x}^{\prime } = s \times  \bar{s} \in  X$。根据定义，$\sqrt{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\mathbf{A}}_{v}}}{\left( {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( s\right)  - {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( t\right) \right) }^{2}} < \sqrt{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\mathbf{A}}_{v}}}{\left( {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( {s}^{\prime }\right)  - {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( t\right) \right) }^{2}}$。由此可得，

$$
\begin{Vmatrix}{x - {\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) }\end{Vmatrix} = \sqrt{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\mathrm{A}}_{u}}}{\left( {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( x\right)  - {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( t\right) \right) }^{2}} = \sqrt{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\mathrm{A}}_{v}}}{\left( {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( x\right)  - {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( t\right) \right) }^{2} + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\mathrm{A}}_{z}}}{\left( {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( x\right)  - {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( t\right) \right) }^{2}}
$$

$$
 = \sqrt{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\mathbf{A}}_{v}}}{\left( {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( {s}^{\prime }\right)  - {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( t\right) \right) }^{2} + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\mathbf{A}}_{z}}}{\left( {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( \bar{s}\right)  - {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( t\right) \right) }^{2}}
$$

$$
 > \sqrt{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\mathrm{A}}_{v}}}{\left( {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( s\right)  - {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( t\right) \right) }^{2} + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\mathrm{A}}_{z}}}{\left( {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( \bar{s}\right)  - {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( t\right) \right) }^{2}} = \sqrt{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{A}_{j} \in  {\mathrm{A}}_{u}}}{\left( {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( {x}^{\prime }\right)  - {\pi }_{{A}_{j}}\left( t\right) \right) }^{2}}
$$

$$
 = \begin{Vmatrix}{{x}^{\prime } - {\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right) }\end{Vmatrix},
$$

which is a contradiction because $x$ is the closest point in $X$ from ${\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right)$ .

这构成矛盾，因为$x$是$X$中离${\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{u}}\left( t\right)$最近的点。

The fifth inequality holds because ${r}_{v},{r}_{z}$ satisfy Equation (5). The sixth inequality holds because of the definition of ${\mathcal{S}}_{v}$ and ${\mathcal{S}}_{z}$ ,i.e., ${\mathbf{v}}_{{\mathcal{S}}_{v}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{v}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{v}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{v}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ (similarly for $z$ ). The seventh inequality holds because ${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{v}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{v}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {\mathbf{v}}_{{O}_{v}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{v}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$ (similarly for $z$ ). The eighth equality holds because it is equivalent to the fourth inequality. The ninth inequality holds because $a + b \leq  \sqrt{2}\sqrt{{a}^{2} + {b}^{2}}$ for $a,b > 0$ . The tenth equality holds because of the definition of the Euclidean metric. The eleventh equality holds by definition of ${\mathbf{v}}_{O}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$ . The last inequality holds because $\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \subseteq  O$ . Overall, $\alpha  = \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \sqrt{2}$ which is a constant. Furthermore,notice that $r = {r}_{v} + {r}_{z}$ so the result follows. Moreover, ${r}_{v} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  q\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }}\begin{Vmatrix}{{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( {{\mathcal{S}}_{v},{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right) }\right) }\end{Vmatrix}$ so $r \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \sqrt{2}{\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) .$

第五不等式成立是由于${r}_{v},{r}_{z}$满足方程(5)。第六不等式成立基于${\mathcal{S}}_{v}$与${\mathcal{S}}_{z}$的定义，即${\mathbf{v}}_{{\mathcal{S}}_{v}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{v}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma {\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{v}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{v}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$（对$z$同理）。第七不等式成立因为${\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{v}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{v}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  {\mathbf{v}}_{{O}_{v}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{v}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right)$（对$z$同理）。第八等式成立因其等价于第四不等式。第九不等式成立源于$a,b > 0$时$a + b \leq  \sqrt{2}\sqrt{{a}^{2} + {b}^{2}}$。第十等式成立依据欧几里得度量的定义。第十一等式成立由${\mathbf{v}}_{O}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)$定义可得。末项不等式成立因为$\operatorname{OPT}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \subseteq  O$。综上，$\alpha  = \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \sqrt{2}$为常数。此外注意$r = {r}_{v} + {r}_{z}$故得结论。更进一步，${r}_{v} \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  q\left( \mathrm{\;D}\right) }}\begin{Vmatrix}{{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( {{\mathcal{S}}_{v},{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right) }\right) }\end{Vmatrix}$因此$r \leq  \left( {1 + \varepsilon }\right) \sqrt{2}{\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) .$

Similarly,we show the analysis assuming $D\mathrm{{GkM}}$ edian ${\mathrm{{Alg}}}_{\gamma }$ is used. We have,

类似地，我们展示采用$D\mathrm{{GkM}}$中位数${\mathrm{{Alg}}}_{\gamma }$时的分析。可得：

$$
{\mathbf{v}}_{X}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right)  \leq  \ldots  \leq  {r}_{v} + {r}_{z}
$$

$$
 \leq  \left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  \mathbf{q}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }}\left( {\begin{Vmatrix}{{\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( {{\operatorname{OPT}}_{\operatorname{disc}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{v}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) ,{\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right) }\right) }\end{Vmatrix} + \begin{Vmatrix}{{\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{z}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( {{\operatorname{OPT}}_{\operatorname{disc}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{z}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) ,{\pi }_{{\mathbf{A}}_{z}}\left( t\right) }\right) }\end{Vmatrix}}\right) }\right) 
$$

$$
 \leq  2\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  q\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }}\left( {\begin{Vmatrix}{{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( {\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{v}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) ,{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{v}}\left( t\right) }\right) }\end{Vmatrix} + \begin{Vmatrix}{{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( t\right)  - \mathcal{N}\left( {\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{z}\left( \mathrm{D}\right) }\right) ,{\pi }_{{\mathrm{A}}_{z}}\left( t\right) }\right) }\end{Vmatrix}}\right) }\right) 
$$

$$
 \leq  \ldots  \leq  2\left( {2 + \varepsilon }\right) \gamma \sqrt{2}{\mathbf{v}}_{\mathrm{{OPT}}\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) }\left( {{\mathbf{q}}_{u}\left( \mathbf{D}\right) }\right) .
$$

The result follows.

由此得证。

## E EXTENSION TO CYCLIC QUERIES

## E 对循环查询的扩展

We first start with some definitions. Let $\mathbf{q}$ be a query over a set of $d$ attributes $\mathbf{A}$ and a set of $m$ relations $\mathbf{R}$ .

首先给出若干定义。设$\mathbf{q}$为针对$\mathbf{A}$属性集$d$及$\mathbf{R}$关系集$m$的查询。

A fractional edge cover of join query $\mathbf{q}$ is a point $x = \left\{  {{x}_{R} \mid  R \in  \mathbf{R}}\right\}   \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{m}$ such that for any attribute $A \in  \mathbf{A},\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{R \in  {\mathbf{R}}_{A}}}{x}_{R} \geq  1$ ,where ${\mathbf{R}}_{A}$ are all the relations in $\mathbf{A}$ that contain the attribute $A$ . As proved in [13],the maximum output size of a join query $\mathbf{q}$ is $O\left( {N}^{\parallel x{\parallel }_{1}}\right)$ . Since the above bound holds for any fractional edge cover,we define $\rho  = \rho \left( \mathbf{q}\right)$ to be the fractional cover with the smallest ${\ell }_{1}$ -norm,i.e., $\rho \left( \mathbf{q}\right)$ is the value of the objective function of the optimal solution of linear programming (LP):

连接查询$\mathbf{q}$的分数边覆盖是一个点$x = \left\{  {{x}_{R} \mid  R \in  \mathbf{R}}\right\}   \in  {\mathbb{R}}^{m}$，使得对于任意属性$A \in  \mathbf{A},\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{R \in  {\mathbf{R}}_{A}}}{x}_{R} \geq  1$（其中${\mathbf{R}}_{A}$是$\mathbf{A}$中包含该属性$A$的所有关系）。如文献[13]所证明，连接查询$\mathbf{q}$的最大输出规模为$O\left( {N}^{\parallel x{\parallel }_{1}}\right)$。由于上述边界条件适用于任何分数边覆盖，我们定义$\rho  = \rho \left( \mathbf{q}\right)$为具有最小${\ell }_{1}$-范数的分数覆盖，即$\rho \left( \mathbf{q}\right)$是线性规划(LP)最优解目标函数的值：

$$
\min \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{R \in  \mathbf{R}}}{x}_{R}\text{,s.t. }\forall R \in  \mathbf{R} : {x}_{R} \geq  0\text{ and }\forall A \in  \mathbf{A} : \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{R \in  {\mathbf{A}}_{R}}}{x}_{R} \geq  1. \tag{9}
$$

Next,we give the definition of the Generalized Hypetree Decomposition (GHD). A GHD of $\mathbf{q}$ is a pair $\left( {\mathcal{T},\lambda }\right)$ ,where $\mathcal{T}$ is a tree as an ordered set of nodes and $\lambda  : \mathcal{T} \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathbf{A}}$ is a labeling function which associates to each vertex $u \in  \mathcal{T}$ a subset of attributes in A,called ${\lambda }_{u}$ ,such that the following conditions are satisfied:

接下来给出广义超树分解(GHD)的定义。$\mathbf{q}$的GHD是一个二元组$\left( {\mathcal{T},\lambda }\right)$，其中$\mathcal{T}$是作为有序节点集的树，$\lambda  : \mathcal{T} \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathbf{A}}$是标记函数，为每个顶点$u \in  \mathcal{T}$关联属性集A的子集（称为${\lambda }_{u}$），且满足以下条件：

- (coverage) For each $R \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,there is a node $u \in  \mathcal{T}$ such that ${\mathbf{A}}_{R} \subseteq  {\lambda }_{u}$ ,where ${\mathbf{A}}_{R}$ is the set of attributes contained in $R$ ;

- (覆盖性) 对于每个$R \in  \mathbf{R}$，存在节点$u \in  \mathcal{T}$使得${\mathbf{A}}_{R} \subseteq  {\lambda }_{u}$（其中${\mathbf{A}}_{R}$是$R$包含的属性集合）；

- (connectivity) For each $A \in  \mathbf{A}$ ,the set of nodes $\left\{  {u \in  \mathcal{T} : A \in  {\lambda }_{u}}\right\}$ forms a connected subtree of $\mathcal{T}$ .

- (连通性) 对于每个$A \in  \mathbf{A}$，节点集$\left\{  {u \in  \mathcal{T} : A \in  {\lambda }_{u}}\right\}$构成$\mathcal{T}$的连通子树。

Given a join query $\mathbf{q}$ ,one of its GHD $\left( {\mathcal{T},\lambda }\right)$ and a node $u \in  \mathcal{T}$ ,the width of $u$ is defined as the optimal fractional edge covering number of its derived hypergraph $\left( {{\lambda }_{u},{\mathcal{E}}_{u}}\right)$ ,where ${\mathcal{E}}_{u} = \left\{  {{\mathbf{A}}_{R} \cap  {\lambda }_{u}}\right.$ : $R \in  \mathbf{R}\}$ . Given a join query and a GHD $\left( {\mathcal{T},\lambda }\right)$ ,the width of $\left( {\mathcal{T},\lambda }\right)$ is defined as the maximum width over all nodes in $\mathcal{T}$ . Then,the fractional hypertree width of a join query follows: The fractional hypertree width of a join query $\mathbf{q}$ ,denoted as $\operatorname{fhw}\left( \mathbf{q}\right)$ ,is

给定连接查询$\mathbf{q}$、其GHD$\left( {\mathcal{T},\lambda }\right)$及节点$u \in  \mathcal{T}$，$u$的宽度定义为其派生超图$\left( {{\lambda }_{u},{\mathcal{E}}_{u}}\right)$的最优分数边覆盖数（其中${\mathcal{E}}_{u} = \left\{  {{\mathbf{A}}_{R} \cap  {\lambda }_{u}}\right.$：$R \in  \mathbf{R}\}$）。对于给定连接查询和GHD$\left( {\mathcal{T},\lambda }\right)$，该GHD的宽度定义为$\mathcal{T}$中所有节点宽度的最大值。因此，连接查询的分数超树宽度定义为：连接查询$\mathbf{q}$的分数超树宽度（记作$\operatorname{fhw}\left( \mathbf{q}\right)$）即

$$
\operatorname{fhw}\left( \mathbf{q}\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{\left( \mathcal{T},\lambda \right) }\mathop{\max }\limits_{{u \in  \mathcal{T}}}\rho \left( {{\lambda }_{u},{\mathcal{E}}_{u}}\right) ,
$$

i.e., the minimum width over all GHDs.

亦即所有GHD中的最小宽度。

Overall, $O\left( {N}^{\text{fhw }}\right)$ is an upper bound on the number of join results materialized for each node in $\mathcal{T}$ . It is also the time complexity to compute the join results for each node in $\mathcal{T}$ [13]. Hence, we converted our original cyclic query into an acyclic join query (with join tree $\mathcal{T}$ ) where each relation has $O\left( {N}^{\text{fhw }}\right)$ tuples. We execute all our algorithms to the new acyclic join query replacing the $N$ factor with the ${N}^{\text{fhw }}$ factor in the running time.

总体而言，$O\left( {N}^{\text{fhw }}\right)$是$\mathcal{T}$中每个节点物化连接结果数量的上限，同时也是计算$\mathcal{T}$中每个节点连接结果的时间复杂度[13]。因此，我们将原始循环查询转换为无环连接查询（其连接树为$\mathcal{T}$），其中每个关系包含$O\left( {N}^{\text{fhw }}\right)$个元组。我们所有算法都针对新的无环连接查询执行，在运行时间中用${N}^{\text{fhw }}$因子替代了$N$因子。
